# GraalScript Comprehensive Guide for LLMs and Dummies
 
## Overview

This guide covers both **GraalScript1 (GS1)** and **GraalScript2 (GS2)**, the scripting languages used in GraalOnline games.

### GraalScript1 (GS1) - Legacy Scripting
- Legacy scripting language used before 2004-2006
- Made obsolete when Graal v4 introduced GS2
- Server-side only scripting
- Simpler syntax and basic functionality
- Still found in old codebases that weren't fully converted

### GraalScript2 (GS2) - Current Scripting  
- Current scripting language introduced with Graal v4
- Client-server architecture with automatic synchronization
- Advanced features and modern syntax
- Enhanced GUI, particle systems, and client-side scripting

### Key Characteristics:
- **Event-driven**: Functions are called automatically when events occur
- **Client-server architecture**: Code can run on client, server, or both
- **Object-oriented**: Uses objects, properties, and methods
- **Dynamic typing**: Variables don't need type declarations
- **String concatenation**: Uses `@` operator instead of `+`
- **Built-in functions**: Extensive library of built-in functions and variables
- **NPC-based**: Scripts run on NPCs (Non-Player Characters) or weapons
- **Class system**: Scripts can be joined from class files using `join()`

## GraalScript1 (GS1) - Legacy Scripting

### GS1 Basic Syntax
GS1 uses simpler syntax without prefixes for most variables:

if (created) { 
  showcharacter; // shows a generic npc
  setcharprop #c, Hello there!; // sets npc chat, setplayerprop is the player equivalent 
  setani sit,; // sets the animation "sit" with no params
}

// Event functions
if (created) {
  // Variables are global by default
  this.health = 100;
  this.maxhealth = 100;
  setcharprop #n, Guard NPC;
  showcharacter;
}

if (actionhurt) {
  // Player damaged this NPC
  this.health -= player.swordpower;
  if (this.health <= 0) {
    setcharprop #c, I'm defeated!;
    destroy;
  } else {
    setcharprop #c, Ouch! Health: #v(this.health) / #v(this.maxhealth);
  }
}

if (actionsword) {
  // Player hit this NPC with sword (GS1 event)
  this.health -= player.swordpower;
  if (this.health <= 0) {
    message I'm defeated!;
    destroy;
  } else {
    message Ouch! I was hit for #v(player.swordpower) damage! Health: #v(this.health) / #v(this.maxhealth);
  }
}
// Note: In GS1, actionsword triggers when the NPC is hit with a sword
// GS1 uses 'message text;' (no parentheses) or 'setcharprop #c, text;' for NPC chat
// GS1 string concatenation uses #v(variable) for variables and #s(string) for strings (not @ operator which is GS2)
// GS2 uses 'message("text");' (with parentheses) and '@' for string concatenation
// NPCs don't have nicknames - use setcharprop #n, to set a display name if needed

// Custom function example (custom functions CAN use parentheses)
if (created) dothing();

function dothing() {
  setcharprop #c, Ouch!;
}

### GS1 Event Functions
onCreated()                    // NPC created
onTimeout()                    // Timer expired
onPlayerEnters()               // Player enters level
onPlayerChats()                // Player sends chat
onPlayerTouchsMe()             // Player touches NPC
onPlayerTouchsOther()          // Player touches another NPC
onPlayerDies()                 // Player dies
onPlayerHurt()                 // Player is hurt
onActionLeftMouse()            // Left mouse click
onActionRightMouse()           // Right mouse click
onActionMiddleMouse()          // Middle mouse click
onActionDoubleMouse()          // Double click
onWasHit()                     // NPC was hit with sword
onWasShot()                    // NPC was shot with arrows
onWasPelt()                    // NPC was pelted
onShotByPlayer()               // NPC was shot by player
onShotByBaddy()                // NPC was shot by baddy
onWeaponFired()                // Weapon fired (D key)
onKeyPressed(keycode, key)     // Key pressed
onMouseDown(mousevalue)        // Mouse button pressed
onMouseUp(mousevalue)          // Mouse button released
onMovementFinished()           // NPC finished moving

### GS1 Built-in Variables
// Core Variables
x                              // NPC's X position
y                              // NPC's Y position
z                              // NPC's Z position (height)
dir                            // NPC's direction
sprite                         // NPC's sprite
ap                             // NPC's alignment points
hearts                         // NPC's heart count
rupees                         // NPC's rupee count
bombs                          // NPC's bomb count
darts                          // NPC's dart count
swordpower                     // NPC's sword power
shieldpower                    // NPC's shield power
glovepower                     // NPC's glove power
timeout                        // NPC's timeout counter
alpha                          // Light intensity for effects
anistep                        // Current animation step
hurtdx                         // X difference when hit
hurtdy                         // Y difference when hit

// Player Variables (when player triggers function)
player.x                       // Player's X position
player.y                       // Player's Y position
player.dir                     // Player's direction
player.sprite                  // Player's sprite
player.hearts                  // Player's heart count
player.rupees                  // Player's rupee count
player.bombs                   // Player's bomb count
player.darts                   // Player's dart count
player.swordpower              // Player's sword power
player.shieldpower             // Player's shield power
player.glovepower              // Player's glove power
player.nick                    // Player's nickname
player.account                 // Player's account name
player.level                   // Player's level
player.chat                    // Player's chat message

// Arrays
npcs[]                         // All NPCs array
players[]                      // All players array
allplayers[]                   // All players array
compus[]                       // Computer opponents array
bombs[]                        // Bombs array
arrows[]                       // Arrows array
items[]                        // Items array
explos[]                       // Explosions array
horses[]                       // Horses array
signs[]                        // Signs array
board[]                        // Board array
tiles[]                        // Tiles array

// System Variables
timevar2                       // Current time in seconds
graalversion                   // Current Graal version
screenwidth                    // Screen width
screenheight                   // Screen height
mousex                         // Mouse X position in level
mousey                         // Mouse Y position in level
mousescreenx                   // Mouse X position on screen
mousescreeny                   // Mouse Y position on screen
mousebuttons                   // Mouse button states
musicpos                       // Music position
musiclen                       // Music length
gravity                        // Projectile gravity

### GS1 Built-in Functions
// Basic NPC Functions
setimg(str)                    // Change NPC image (TServerNPC.setimg)
setimgpart(str, int, int, int, int) // Set image part (TServerNPC.setimgpart)
hide()                         // Hide NPC
show()                         // Show NPC
destroy()                      // Delete NPC
showcharacter()                // Show generic NPC character (TServerNPC.showcharacter)
sleep(float)                   // Pause script execution for time seconds
message(str)                   // Display text over NPC (TServerNPC.message) - Sets the chat of the calling entity (NPC's chat if called from NPC script, player's chat if called from player/weapon script)
echo(str)                      // Output to log (same as trace(str))
trace(str)                     // Output to log (same as echo(str))
say(int)                       // Display signs by sign index (TServerPlayer.say)
say2(str)                      // Display text as sign (TServerPlayer.say2)
lay(str)                       // Lay an item (server-side)
take(str)                      // Take an item (TServerNPC.take, clientside)
take2(int)                     // Take item by index (TServerNPC.take2)
move(float, float, float, int) // Move NPC smoothly (TServerNPC.move), params: delta x, delta y, time, options (0/1/2=cache type, +4=blockcheck, +8=eventwhendone, +16=applydir)
setcharani(str, str)           // Set animation (TServerNPC.setcharani), params: ganifile, params
level.putnpc2(float, float, str) // Create NPC, params: x, y, scriptname (returns object)
putnpc2(float, float, str)     // Create NPC at x, y with script (TServerLevel.putnpc2), returns object
callnpc(int, params...)         // Call another NPC's script by index with parameters
callweapon(int, params...)      // Call weapon script by index with parameters (TServerPlayer.callweapon deprecated)
carryobject(str)                // Carry object
throwcarry()                   // Throw carried object
seteffect(float, float, float, float) // Set day/night effect (clientside), params: red, green, blue, alpha (use seteffectmode(int) to set mode)
setcoloreffect(float, float, float, float) // Set color effect (TServerNPC.setcoloreffect, clientside), params: red, green, blue, alpha (0-1)
setzoomeffect(float)          // Set zoom effect (TServerNPC.setzoomeffect, clientside), params: zoomfactor (>1 = zoom, <1 = shrink)
showani(int, float, float, int, str) // Show animation (TServerNPC.showani), returns object, params: index, x, y, direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right), animation
showani2(int, float, float, float, int, str) // Show animation with z (TServerNPC.showani2), returns object
showpoly(int, obj)             // Draw polygon (clientside TServerNPC.showpoly), returns object, params: index, coordinates array {x1,y1,x2,y2,...}
showpoly2(int, obj)            // Draw polygon variant (clientside TServerNPC.showpoly2), returns object
showtext(int, float, float, str, str, str) // Show text (TServerNPC.showtext), returns object, params: index, x, y, font, style, text
showtext2(int, float, float, float, str, str, str) // Show text with z (TServerNPC.showtext2), returns object
showimg(int, str, float, float) // Show image (TServerNPC.showimg), returns object, params: index, filename, x, y
showimg2(int, str, float, float, float) // Show image with z (TServerNPC.showimg2), returns object
hideimg(int)                   // Hide image by index
hideimgs(int, int)              // Hide multiple images, params: indexstart, indexend
changeimgpart(int, int, int, int, int) // Change image part, params: index, x, y, width, height
changeimgvis(int, int)          // Change image visibility, params: index, drawingheight
changeimgcolors(int, float, float, float, float) // Change image colors, params: index, red, green, blue, alpha
changeimgzoom(int, float)       // Change image zoom, params: index, zoomfactor
changeimgmode(int, int)         // Change image mode, params: index, mode
setshape(int, int, int)         // Set NPC shape, params: type, width, height
setshape2(int, int, obj)        // Set NPC shape to tiles, params: width, height, tiletypes array
shoot(float, float, float, float, float, float, str, str) // Shoot projectile (TServerLevel.shoot), params: x, y, z, angle, zangle, strength, ani, aniparams
shootarrow(int)                // Shoot arrow (clientside TServerNPC.shootarrow only), params: direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)
shootfireball(int)             // Shoot fireball (TServerNPC.shootfireball), params: direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)
shootfireblast(int)            // Shoot fireblast (TServerNPC.shootfireblast), params: direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)
shootnuke(int)                 // Shoot nuke (TServerNPC.shootnuke), params: direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)
shootball()                    // Shoot ball to player (TServerNPC.shootball)
spyfire(float, float)          // Shoot horse fire, params: length, power
join(str)                      // Join class and inherit functions/event handlers (TGraalVar.join)
joinedclasses                  // Object with joined classes (TGraalVar.joinedclasses)
isinclass(str)                 // Check if object joined class, returns boolean (TGraalVar.isinclass)
leave(str)                     // Leave previously joined class (TGraalVar.leave)
hitplayer(int, float, float, float) // Hurt player (clientside), params: player index, halfhearts, fromx, fromy
hitnpc(int, float, float, float) // Hurt NPC, params: NPC index, halfhearts, fromx, fromy
takehorse(int)                 // Take horse by index
warpto(str, float, float)      // Warp NPC to level (TServerNPC.warpto), params: level filename, x, y
wraptext(length, delims, text) // Wrap text
wraptext2(width, zoom, delims, text) // Wrap text to pixel width

#### TServerPlayer Object Properties (Server-side)

When you access a player object (via `findplayer()`, `findplayer2()`, etc.), you get a `TServerPlayer` object with many properties:

**Basic Player Information:**
- `player.account` - string (read-only) - Player's account name
- `player.communityname` - string (read-only) - Player's community name
- `player.nick` - string - Player's displayed nickname
- `player.chat` - string - Player's chat message (can be set)
- `player.guild` - string - Player's guild name
- `player.graalversion` - float (read-only) - Client version number
- `player.level` - string - Current level filename
- `player.dir` - integer - Direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)

**Position and Movement:**
- `player.x` - float - X coordinate
- `player.y` - float - Y coordinate

**Stats and Resources:**
- `player.hearts` - float - Current hearts (health)
- `player.fullhearts` - integer - Maximum hearts
- `player.rupees` - integer - Rupee count
- `player.bombs` - integer - Bomb count
- `player.arrows` - integer - Arrow count
- `player.darts` - integer - Dart count
- `player.ap` - integer - Action points/energy
- `player.gralats` - integer - Gralat currency
- `player.deaths` - integer - Death count

**Equipment and Appearance:**
- `player.head` - string - Head image
- `player.headimg` - string - Head image (alternative)
- `player.body` - string - Body image
- `player.bodyimg` - string - Body image (alternative)
- `player.sword` - string - Sword image
- `player.shield` - string - Shield image
- `player.swordpower` - integer - Sword damage power
- `player.shieldpower` - integer - Shield defense power
- `player.glovepower` - integer - Glove/grab power
- `player.horseimg` - string - Horse image
- `player.headset` - integer - Headset image index

**Animation:**
- `player.ani` - string - Current animation
- `player.aniparams` - string - Animation parameters

**Attachment and Carrying:**
- `player.attached` - boolean (read-only) - Is player attached to object
- `player.attachid` - integer (read-only) - Attachment object ID
- `player.attachtype` - integer (read-only) - Attachment type
- `player.carriesstone` - boolean (read-only) - Carrying stone
- `player.carriesbush` - boolean (read-only) - Carrying bush
- `player.carriesvase` - boolean (read-only) - Carrying vase
- `player.carriesnpc` - boolean (read-only) - Carrying NPC
- `player.carriessign` - boolean (read-only) - Carrying sign
- `player.carriesblackstone` - boolean (read-only) - Carrying black stone

**Admin and Rights:**
- `player.adminlevel` - Note: This is not a standard built-in property. Use server-specific admin checking methods.
- `player.adminworlds` - string (read-only) - Worlds player has admin in
- `player.hasright(str, str)` - returns boolean - Check file rights (rights string, filename)
- `player.hasrightflag(str)` - returns boolean - Check admin flag (e.g., "warptoxy", "warpplayers", etc.)

**Technical Properties:**
- `player.computerid` - integer (read-only) - Computer ID (authorized servers only)
- `player.computerfirstlogin` - integer (read-only) - First login timestamp
- `player.canspin` - boolean - Can player spin

**Player Methods:**
- `player.addweapon(str)` - Add weapon to player
- `player.findweapon(str)` - returns object - Find weapon by name
- `player.attachplayertoobj(int, int)` - Attach player to object
- `player.detachplayer()` - Detach player from object
- `player.freezeplayer2()` - Freeze player movement (server-side)
- `player.enableweapons()` - Enable player weapons
- `player.disableweapons()` - Disable player weapons
- `player.destroy()` - Delete player (only for script-created players)
- `player.callweapon(int, params...)` - DEPRECATED: Call weapon script
- `player.getpersonaluploadfolder()` - returns string - Get player's upload folder path
- `player.hasright(str, str)` - returns boolean - Check file access rights
- `player.hasrightflag(str)` - returns boolean - Check admin flag

**Admin Flags for hasrightflag():**
- `"warptoxy"` - Can warp to coordinates
- `"warptoplayer"` - Can warp to player
- `"warpplayers"` - Can warp other players
- `"updatelevel"` - Can update level
- `"disconnectplayers"` - Can disconnect players
- `"viewattributes"` - Can view player attributes
- `"setattributes"` - Can set player attributes
- `"setownattributes"` - Can set own attributes
- `"resetattributes"` - Can reset attributes
- `"adminmessage"` - Can send admin messages
- `"changerights"` - Can change file rights
- `"banplayers"` - Can ban players
- `"changecomments"` - Can change comments
- `"invisible"` - Invisible mode
- `"changestaffaccounts"` - Can change staff accounts
- `"setserverflags"` - Can set server flags
- `"changeoptions"` - Can change server options
- `"changefolderconfig"` - Can change folder config
- `"changefolderrights"` - Can change folder rights
- `"NPC-Control"` - Can control NPCs

**Example Usage:**

```gs2
// Server-side player manipulation
function onCreated() {
  // Find a player
  temp.pl = findplayer("SomePlayer");
  if (temp.pl != null) {
    // Check admin level
    // Note: player.adminlevel is not a standard built-in property
    // Use server-specific admin checking methods
    if (pl.account == "admin" || pl.nick == "Admin") {
      temp.pl.chat = "You are an admin!";
    }
    
    // Check rights
    if (temp.pl.hasright("rw", "levels/mylevel.nw")) {
      temp.pl.chat = "You have read/write access to mylevel.nw";
    }
    
    // Modify stats
    temp.pl.hearts = 5.5;
    temp.pl.rupees += 100;
    temp.pl.bombs = 10;
    
    // Change appearance
    temp.pl.head = "head_blue.png";
    temp.pl.body = "body_red.png";
    temp.pl.sword = "sword_iron.png";
    temp.pl.swordpower = 5;
    
    // Attach to object
    temp.pl.attachplayertoobj(5, 1);
    
    // Freeze player
    temp.pl.freezeplayer2();
    
    // Check what player is carrying
    if (temp.pl.carriesstone) {
      temp.pl.chat = "You're carrying a stone!";
    }
    
    // Warp player
    setlevel2("levels/town.nw", 10.5, 20.3);
  }
}
```

toinventory()                   // Add NPC to player inventory (GS1 ONLY - does not exist in GS2)
toweapons()                     // Add weapon to player weapons (GS1 ONLY - does not exist in GS2)

// Historical Context for toinventory() and toweapons():
// These functions were used before dedicated NPC servers existed. Weapons had to be
// obtained from levels where scripts with weapons would use toinventory or toweapons
// to give items/NPCs to players. These functions are now deprecated but still work.
// Modern servers use NPC servers which allow adding/updating code remotely without
// needing level-based scripts. The addweapon() function is similar to toweapons()
// but takes the weapon script from the database rather than the current NPC.

// Communication Functions
sendtorc(str)                  // Send message to RC chat window (visible only to this player)
sendtonc(str)                  // Send message to NC
sendpm(str)                    // Send PM to player (TServerPlayer.sendpm(str))
sendrpgmessage(str)            // Send RPG message to log window (F2) of player (TServerPlayer.sendrpgmessage(str))
findplayer(str)                 // Find player by name, returns object
findplayer2(str)                // Case insensitive find, returns object (same as findplayerbycommunityname)
findplayerbycommunityname(str) // Find player by account or community name, returns object

findnearestplayers(float, float) // Find nearest players, returns object
findareaplayers(float, float, float, float) // Find players in rectangle (TServerLevel.findareaplayers), returns object, params: x, y, width, height

#### TServerNPC Object Properties (Server-side)

When you access an NPC (via `findnpc()`, `npcs[]`, etc.), you get a `TServerNPC` object with many properties:

**Basic NPC Information:**
- `npc.id` - integer (read-only) - NPC index/ID
- `npc.nick` - string - NPC nickname/display name
- `npc.chat` - string - NPC chat message
- `npc.level` - string - Current level filename
- `npc.dir` - integer - Direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)
- `npc.x` - float - X coordinate
- `npc.y` - float - Y coordinate
- `npc.height` - float (read-only) - NPC height

**Stats and Resources:**
- `npc.hearts` - float - Current hearts (health)
- `npc.fullhearts` - integer - Maximum hearts
- `npc.rupees` - integer - Rupee count
- `npc.bombs` - integer - Bomb count
- `npc.arrows` - integer - Arrow count
- `npc.darts` - integer - Dart count
- `npc.ap` - integer - Action points/energy
- `npc.gralats` - integer - Gralat currency
- `npc.swordpower` - integer - Sword damage power
- `npc.shieldpower` - integer - Shield defense power
- `npc.glovepower` - integer - Glove/grab power

**Equipment and Appearance:**
- `npc.head` - string - Head image
- `npc.headimg` - string - Head image (alternative)
- `npc.body` - string - Body image
- `npc.bodyimg` - string - Body image (alternative)
- `npc.sword` - string - Sword image
- `npc.shield` - string - Shield image
- `npc.horseimg` - string - Horse image
- `npc.image` - string - NPC image
- `npc.ani` - string - Current animation
- `npc.aniparams` - string - Animation parameters

**Movement and Blocking:**
- `npc.isblocking` - boolean - Is NPC blocking path
- `npc.hurtdx` - float - X knockback direction
- `npc.hurtdy` - float - Y knockback direction
- `npc.hurtpower` - float (read-only) - Last hit power

**NPC Behavior:**
- `npc.dontsave` - boolean - Don't save NPC state
- `npc.hidetoclients` - boolean - Hide from clients
- `npc.canwarp()` - Can NPC warp
- `npc.canwarp2()` - Can NPC warp (variant)
- `npc.cannotwarp()` - Cannot NPC warp
- `npc.canbecarried()` - NPC can be carried
- `npc.cannotbecarried()` - NPC cannot be carried
- `npc.canbepushed()` - NPC can be pushed
- `npc.cannotbepushed()` - NPC cannot be pushed
- `npc.canbepulled()` - NPC can be pulled
- `npc.cannotbepulled()` - NPC cannot be pulled

**NPC Methods:**
- `npc.destroy()` - Delete NPC
- `npc.hide()` - Hide NPC
- `npc.show()` - Show NPC
- `npc.dontblock()` - Don't block path
- `npc.blockagain()` - Block path again
- `npc.drawoverplayer()` - Draw NPC over players
- `npc.drawunderplayer()` - Draw NPC under players
- `npc.drawaslight()` - Draw NPC as light source
- `npc.findimg(int)` - returns object - Find image by index
- `npc.hideimg(int)` - Hide image by index
- `npc.hideimgs(int, int)` - Hide multiple images
- `npc.changeimgcolors(int, float, float, float, float)` - Change image colors
- `npc.changeimgmode(int, int)` - Change image mode
- `npc.changeimgpart(int, int, int, int, int)` - Change image part
- `npc.changeimgvis(int, int)` - Change image visibility
- `npc.changeimgzoom(int, float)` - Change image zoom

**NPC Image Functions:**
- `npc.showimg(int, str, float, float)` - Show image, returns object
- `npc.showimg2(int, str, float, float, float)` - Show image with Z, returns object
- `npc.showani(int, float, float, int, str)` - Show animation, returns object
- `npc.showpoly(int, obj)` - Draw polygon, returns object

**Example Usage:**

```gs2
// Server-side NPC manipulation
function onCreated() {
  // Find NPC by name
  temp.npc = findnpc("GuardNPC");
  // Or find by index (if you know the name):
  // temp.npcIndex = level.getnpcindex("GuardNPC");
  // temp.npc = findnpcbyid(npcIndex); // Clientside only
  if (temp.npc != null) {
    // Modify NPC appearance
    temp.npc.head = "head_guard.png";
    temp.npc.body = "body_armor.png";
    temp.npc.sword = "sword_iron.png";
    temp.npc.swordpower = 5;
    
    // Set NPC stats
    temp.npc.hearts = 10.0;
    temp.npc.fullhearts = 10;
    temp.npc.rupees = 50;
    
    // Control NPC visibility
    temp.npc.hidetoclients = false;
    temp.npc.drawoverplayer();
    
    // Control NPC blocking
    if (temp.npc.isblocking) {
      temp.npc.dontblock();
    }
    
    // Check NPC properties
    if (temp.npc.canwarp()) {
      temp.npc.warpto("levels/town.nw", 10, 20);
    }
    
    // Destroy NPC
    // temp.npc.destroy();
  }
  
  // Iterate all NPCs
  for (temp.npc in npcs) {
    if (temp.npc.nick == "Guard") {
      temp.npc.chat = "Hello!";
    }
  }
}
```

#### TServerLevel Object Properties and Methods

The `level` object (and `findlevel()`) provides access to level properties and methods:

**Level Properties:**
- `level.width` - integer (read-only) - Level width in tiles
- `level.height` - integer (read-only) - Level height in tiles
- `level.tilemap` - object (read-only) - Tile map object (new level format)
- `level.tilelayers` - object (read-only) - Tile layers array
- `level.tilelayercount` - integer - Number of tile layers
- `level.board` - object (read-only) - Level board/background

**Level Zone Flags:**
- `level.isjail` - boolean (read-only) - Is jail level (from server option)
- `level.issingleplayer` - boolean (read-only) - Is single-player level (from server option)
- `level.issparringzone` - boolean (read-only) - Is sparring zone
- `level.isnopkzone` - boolean - Is no-PK zone (clientside: `nopkzone`)
- `level.compsdead` - boolean (read-only) - All computer opponents dead (clientside)

**Level Collision Detection:**
- `level.onwall(float, float)` - returns boolean - Check if point is on wall
- `level.onwall2(float, float, float, float)` - returns boolean - Check if rectangle is on wall
- `level.onwallpath(float, float, float, float, float, float)` - returns boolean - Check if path intersects wall
- `level.onwater(float, float)` - returns boolean - Check if point is on water
- `level.onwater2(float, float, float, float)` - returns boolean - Check if rectangle is on water
- `level.onwaterpath(float, float, float, float, float, float)` - returns boolean - Check if path intersects water
- `level.tiletype(float, float)` - returns integer - Get tile type at position
- `level.getz(float, float)` - returns float - Get Z height at position

**Level NPC/Player Finding:**
- `level.findareanpcs(float, float, float, float)` - returns object - Find NPCs in rectangle (x, y, width, height)
- `level.findareaplayers(float, float, float, float)` - returns object - Find players in rectangle, uses blocking rect (0.5,1,2,2)
- `level.getareanpcs(float, float, float, float)` - returns object - Get NPCs in area
- `level.testnpc(float, float)` - returns integer - Test NPC at position
- `level.testplayer(float, float)` - returns integer - Test player at position
- `level.getnpcindex(str)` - returns integer - Get NPC index by name

**Level Projectile/Effects:**
- `shoot(float, float, float, float, float, float, str, str)` - Shoot projectile (global function, not level.shoot()), params: x, y, z, angle, zangle, strength, ani, aniparams
- `level.putbomb(int, float, float)` - Place bomb (TServerLevel.putbomb), params: power, x, y
- `level.putbomb2(int, float, float, str)` - Place bomb with custom image, params: power, x, y, image
- `level.updateboard(int, int, int, int)` - Update board display, params: x, y, width, height
- `level.updateboard2(int, int, int, int)` - Update board with tile changes, params: x, y, width, height
- `savelevel(str)` - Force level to save immediately (use after updateboard2 tile modifications)
- `level.putbomb2(int, float, float, str)` - Place bomb with image, params: power, x, y, image
- `level.putexplosion(int, float, float)` - Place explosion, params: power, x, y
- `level.putexplosion2(int, int, float, float)` - Place explosion variant, params: power1, power2, x, y

**Level Actions:**
- `level.triggeraction(float, float, str, str)` - Trigger action at position, params: x, y, action, params
- `level.hitobjects(float, float, float)` - Hit objects at position, params: x, y, power

**Level Board/Display:**
- `level.updateboard(int, int, int, int)` - Update board area, params: x, y, width, height
- `level.updateboard2(int, int, int, int)` - Update board area variant

**Level File Operations:**
- `level.save(float, float)` - Save level at position, params: x, y
- `level.getmappartfile(float, float)` - returns string - Get map part filename at position
- `level.dontswapoutlevel()` - Don't swap/unload level from memory

**Clientside Level Methods:**
- `level.putcomp(str, float, float)` - Place computer opponent, params: script, x, y
- `level.putnewcomp(str, float, float, str, int)` - Place new computer opponent, params: script, x, y, image, index
- `level.puthorse(str, float, float)` - Place horse, params: image, x, y
- `level.hitcompu(int, int, float, float)` - Hit computer opponent, params: index1, index2, x, y
- `level.reflectarrow(int)` - Reflect arrow, params: index
- `level.removearrow(int)` - Remove arrow, params: index

**Example Usage:**

```gs2
// Level collision detection
function canMoveTo(x, y) {
  if (level.onwall(x, y)) {
    return false;  // Blocked by wall
  }
  if (level.onwater(x, y)) {
    return true;  // Can walk on water (or return false to block)
  }
  return true;
}

// Find players in area
function findPlayersInShop() {
  temp.players = level.findareaplayers(10, 10, 20, 20);  // Rectangle 10,10 with 20x20 size
  for (temp.pl in players) {
    temp.pl.chat = "Welcome to the shop!";
  }
}

// Level zone checks
function onCreated() {
  if (level.isnopkzone) {
    chat = "This is a safe zone - no PK allowed";
  }
  if (level.issparringzone) {
    chat = "Sparring zone - duels only";
  }
}

// Shoot projectile from level
function shootAtTarget(targetX, targetY) {
  temp.dx = targetX - x;
  temp.dy = targetY - y;
  temp.angle = atan2(temp.dy, temp.dx);
  shoot(x, y, 0, temp.angle, 0, 5, "arrow", "");
}

// Check tile type
function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  temp.tile = level.tiletype(player.x, player.y);
  if (temp.tile == 5) {
    player.chat = "You're standing on a special tile!";
  }
}
```

### Common Enumerations

// Direction Values
// Used in: setplayerdir(), shootarrow(), shootfireball(), showani(), etc.
0 = Up
1 = Left
2 = Down
3 = Right

// Key Values (keydown)
// Used in: keydown(keyvalue)
0 = Up arrow
1 = Left arrow
2 = Down arrow
3 = Right arrow
4 = D key
5 = S key
6 = A key
7 = M key
8 = Tab key
9 = Q key
10 = P key

// Image Drawing Modes
// Used in: changeimgmode(), seteffectmode()
0 = Add (light blending)
1 = Transparent (normal alpha blending)
2 = Subtract (dark blending)
3 = Day/night (lighting effect)

// Move Options (TServerNPC.move)
// Used in: move(float, float, float, int) - options parameter is bitwise flags
0 = Default (no special options)
1 = Cache type
2 = Append cache
+4 = Block check (check for collisions)
+8 = Event when done (trigger event on completion)
+16 = Apply direction (set NPC direction based on movement)

// Save Modes (savelines)
// Used in: savelines(str, int)
0 = Overwrite (replace existing file)
1 = Append (add to existing file)

### GS1 Practical Examples

#### Basic NPC with Health System
function onCreated() {
  health = 100;
  maxhealth = 100;
  nick = "Guard";
  setimg("guard.png");
}

function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  if (player.swordpower > 0) {
    health -= 10;
    if (health <= 0) {
      chat = "I'm defeated!";
      destroy();
    } else {
      chat = "Ouch! Health: " @ health @ "/" @ maxhealth;
    }
  }
}

function onWasHit() {
  health -= 5;
  if (health <= 0) {
    chat = "I'm defeated!";
    destroy();
  }
}

#### Shop NPC
function onCreated() {
  nick = "Shop Keeper";
  setimg("shopkeeper.png");
}

function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  if (player.rupees >= 50) {
    player.rupees -= 50;
    player.bombs += 1;
    chat = "Here's a bomb! You have " @ player.rupees @ " rupees left.";
  } else {
    chat = "You need 50 rupees for a bomb!";
  }
}

#### Weapon with Ammo System
function onCreated() {
  // Use player.darts or player.arrows for arrow count
  player.darts = 10;
  player.maxdarts = 10;
}

function onWeaponFired() {
  if (player.darts > 0) {
    player.darts--;
    shootarrow(player.dir);
    chat = "Arrows: " @ player.darts @ "/" @ player.maxdarts;
  } else {
    chat = "Out of arrows!";
  }
}

function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  if (player.rupees >= 25) {
    player.rupees -= 25;
    player.darts = player.maxdarts;
    chat = "Arrows refilled!";
  } else {
    chat = "Need 25 rupees for arrows!";
  }
}

#### Timer-based NPC
function onCreated() {
  setTimer(5);
  chat = "I'll say something in 5 seconds...";
}

function onTimeout() {
  chat = "Time's up!";
  setTimer(3);
}

function onTimeout() {
  chat = "Another 3 seconds passed!";
  setTimer(2);
}

function onTimeout() {
  chat = "Final message!";
}

### GS1 Advanced Features

#### Custom Functions and Classes
GS1 supports custom functions through the class system:

// In a class file (e.g., functions_math.txt)
public function addCommas(num) {
  temp.str = "";
  temp.myNum = int(num);
  temp.myNumLength = myNum.length();
  
  for (temp.i = 0; i < myNumLength; i ++) {
    temp.char = myNum.charat(i);
    if ((myNumLength - i) % 3 == 0 && i > 0) {
      str @= ",";
    }
    str @= char;
  }
  return str;
}

public function formatNumbers(num) {
  // Format 1000 -> 1K, 150100 -> 150.10K, etc.
  temp.str = "";
  temp.myNum = int(num);
  temp.myNumLength = myNum.length();
  
  for (temp.i = 0; i < myNumLength; i ++) {
    temp.char = myNum.charat(i);
    if ((myNumLength - i) % 3 == 0 && i > 0) {
      str @= ".";
      temp.c ++;
    }
    str @= char;
  }
  
  if (c > 0) {
    temp.toks = str.tokenize(".");
    if (c = 1) {
      return toks[0] @ "." @ toks[1].substring(0,2) @ "K";
    }
    if (c = 2) {
      return toks[0] @ "." @ toks[1].substring(0,2) @ "M";
    }
    if (c = 3) {
      return toks[0] @ "." @ toks[1].substring(0,2) @ "B";
    }
  }
  return str;
}

function roundTo(val, place) {
  return int(val/place + 0.5)*place;
}

#### Cooldown System
// In functions_cooldown.txt
function Cooldown(temp.cdname, temp.cddur) {
  // If no duration, check if cooldown is active
  if (temp.cddur == null) {
    return this.("cooldown_" @ temp.cdname);
  }
  // Set cooldown
  else {
    if (this.("cooldown_" @ temp.cdname) != 1) {
      this.("cooldown_" @ temp.cdname) = 1;
      scheduleevent(temp.cddur, "ResetCooldown", temp.cdname);
    }
  }
}

function onResetCooldown(temp.cdname) {
  this.("cooldown_" @ temp.cdname) = 0;
}

#### Projectile System
// Advanced projectile handling
function onActionProjectile2(hitx, hity, type, acct, p4, p5, p6, p7, p8) {
  if (type == "joeyshuriken") {
    temp.img = showimg(1, "nmd_shuriken1.gif", hitx-1.4, hity-1.5);
    temp.img.zoom = 3/4;
    temp.img.rotation = random(0, 2*pi);
    temp.img.layer = 20;
    scheduleevent(1, "destroyimg", 1);
  }
  else if (type == "lasergun") {
    temp.duration = 2;
    if (p5 == 2) temp.duration = 4;
    temp.img = showimg(2, "g4_particle_sun.png", hitx-2, hity-2);
    temp.img.zoom = sin((max(0.35, p5)*(pi/2))/2);
    if (p5 == 2) temp.img.zoom = 2;
    temp.img.red = 0;
    temp.img.green = 1;
    temp.img.blue = 1/3;
    temp.img.alpha = 0.999;
    temp.img.mode = 0;
    temp.img.rotation = random(0, 2*pi);
    temp.img.layer = 20;
    scheduleevent(temp.duration, "destroyimg", 2);
  }
}

function onEventdestroyimg(imgid) {
  hideimg(imgid);
}

function dist(x1, y1, x2, y2) {
  return ((x2-x1)^2 + (y2-y1)^2)^.5;
}

#### Advanced NPC Examples

##### Baddy AI System
function onCreated() {
  health = 100;
  maxhealth = 100;
  nick = "Baddy";
  setimg("baddy.png");
  setTimer(0.1);
  ai_state = "patrol";
  patrol_direction = 1;
  last_player_x = 0;
  last_player_y = 0;
}

function onTimeout() {
  if (ai_state == "patrol") {
    // Move in patrol direction
    if (onwall(x + patrol_direction, y)) {
      patrol_direction = -patrol_direction;
    } else {
      x += patrol_direction;
    }
  }
  else if (ai_state == "chase") {
    // Chase player
    temp.target = getnearestplayer(x, y);
    if (target != null) {
      temp.dx = target.x - x;
      temp.dy = target.y - y;
      temp.distance = dist(x, y, target.x, target.y);
      
      if (distance > 1) {
        x += dx / distance;
        y += dy / distance;
      }
      
      if (distance < 2) {
        ai_state = "attack";
        setTimer(1);
      }
    } else {
      ai_state = "patrol";
      setTimer(0.1);
    }
  }
  else if (ai_state == "attack") {
    // Attack nearby player
    temp.target = getnearestplayer(x, y);
    if (target != null) {
      hitplayer(target, 1, x, y);
    }
    ai_state = "chase";
    setTimer(0.1);
  }
  
  setTimer(0.1);
}

function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  ai_state = "chase";
  setTimer(0.1);
}

##### Interactive Shop System
function onCreated() {
  nick = "Shop Keeper";
  setimg("shopkeeper.png");
  shop_items = {
    "Sword": 100,
    "Shield": 75,
    "Bomb": 25,
    "Arrow": 5
  };
  current_selection = 0;
  showShopMenu();
}

function showShopMenu() {
  temp.menu_text = "Shop Menu:\n";
  // Note: getstringkeys() only works on variable prefixes (e.g., "this.shop_items_")
  // For temp variables, manually track keys or use numeric indices
  temp.item_names = {"sword", "potion", "shield", "bow"}; // Manually define keys
  // Or store keys when building shop_items:
  // this.shop_items["sword"] = 100;
  // this.shop_items["potion"] = 50;
  // Then: temp.keys = getstringkeys("this.shop_items"); (but this requires this.shop_items_ prefix)
  
  for (temp.i = 0; i < item_names.size(); i++) {
    temp.item = item_names[i];
    temp.price = shop_items[item];
    if (i == current_selection) {
      menu_text @= "> " @ item @ " - " @ price @ " rupees\n";
    } else {
      menu_text @= "  " @ item @ " - " @ price @ " rupees\n";
    }
  }
  
  say2(menu_text);
}

function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  if (keydown(4)) { // D key
    // Use manually tracked item_names array from showShopMenu()
    temp.selected_item = item_names[current_selection];
    temp.price = shop_items[selected_item];
    
    if (player.rupees >= price) {
      player.rupees -= price;
      if (selected_item == "Sword") {
        player.swordpower += 1;
      } else if (selected_item == "Shield") {
        player.shieldpower += 1;
      } else if (selected_item == "Bomb") {
        player.bombs += 1;
      } else if (selected_item == "Arrow") {
        player.darts += 10;
      }
      chat = "You bought " @ selected_item @ " for " @ price @ " rupees!";
    } else {
      chat = "You need " @ price @ " rupees for " @ selected_item @ "!";
    }
  }
  else if (keydown(0)) { // Up arrow
    current_selection--;
    if (current_selection < 0) {
      current_selection = item_names.size() - 1;
    }
    showShopMenu();
  }
  else if (keydown(2)) { // Down arrow
    current_selection++;
    if (current_selection >= item_names.size()) {
      current_selection = 0;
    }
    showShopMenu();
  }
}

##### Quest System
function onCreated() {
  nick = "Quest Master";
  setimg("questmaster.png");
  quest_active = false;
  quest_step = 0;
  quest_items_needed = {"Red Gem": 3, "Blue Gem": 2};
  quest_items_collected = {"Red Gem": 0, "Blue Gem": 0};
}

function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  if (!quest_active) {
    chat = "Welcome! I need you to collect gems for me. Will you help?";
    quest_active = true;
    quest_step = 1;
  } else {
    checkQuestProgress();
  }
}

function checkQuestProgress() {
  temp.all_collected = true;
  temp.progress_text = "Quest Progress:\n";
  
  // Manually track quest item keys
  temp.quest_items = {"Red Gem", "Blue Gem"};
  
  for (temp.i = 0; i < quest_items.size(); i++) {
    temp.item = quest_items[i];
    temp.needed = quest_items_needed[item];
    temp.collected = quest_items_collected[item];
    progress_text @= item @ ": " @ collected @ "/" @ needed @ "\n";
    
    if (collected < needed) {
      all_collected = false;
    }
  }
  
  if (all_collected) {
    chat = "Excellent! You've completed the quest! Here's your reward!";
    player.rupees += 500;
    player.swordpower += 1;
    quest_active = false;
    quest_step = 0;
    // Reset quest items
    for (temp.i = 0; i < quest_items.size(); i++) {
      temp.item = quest_items[i];
      quest_items_collected[item] = 0;
    }
  } else {
    say2(progress_text);
  }
}

function onPlayerTouchsOther(other) {
  // Check if player is touching a gem
  if (other.nick == "Red Gem") {
    quest_items_collected["Red Gem"]++;
    other.destroy();
    chat = "You collected a Red Gem!";
  } else if (other.nick == "Blue Gem") {
    quest_items_collected["Blue Gem"]++;
    other.destroy();
    chat = "You collected a Blue Gem!";
  }
}

## GraalScript2 (GS2) - Modern Scripting

### GS2 Particle Emitters

Particle emitters are advanced visual effects in GS2 that create dynamic particle systems. They are attached to images created with `showimg()`.

#### Basic Emitter Setup
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  showimg(1, "block.png", player.x, player.y);
  with (findimg(1)) {
    layer = 1; // Draw under player
    x = player.x;
    y = player.y;
    
    // Emitter configuration
    emitter.attachposition = true; // Move with image
    emitter.delaymin = 0.1; // Min delay between emissions
    emitter.delaymax = 0.3; // Max delay between emissions
    emitter.emissionoffset = "{0, 0, 0}"; // Offset from x,y,z
    emitter.firstinfront = false; // First particle in front
    emitter.nrofparticles = 1; // Particles per emission
    emitter.maxparticles = 50; // Max particles at once
    
    // Particle properties
    emitter.particle.lifetime = 2.0; // How long particles last
    emitter.particle.image = "sparkle.png"; // Particle image
    emitter.particle.mode = 0; // 0=add, 1=trans, 2=subtract
    emitter.particle.alpha = 0.8; // Transparency (0-1)
    emitter.particle.zoom = 1.0; // Size multiplier
    emitter.particle.red = 1.0; // Red color (0-1)
    emitter.particle.green = 0.5; // Green color (0-1)
    emitter.particle.blue = 0.0; // Blue color (0-1)
    emitter.particle.zangle = 0; // Upward angle (0 to 2*pi)
    emitter.particle.angle = 0; // Direction angle (0 to 2*pi)
    emitter.particle.speed = 2.0; // Movement speed
    emitter.particle.spin = 1.0; // Rotations during lifetime
    emitter.particle.stretchx = 1.0; // Width stretch
    emitter.particle.stretchy = 1.0; // Height stretch
  }
}

#### Emitter Modifiers

Particle modifiers change particle properties over time. There are three types of modifiers:

**Modifier Functions:**

`addlocalmodifier(str, float, float, str, str, float, float)` - returns object
- Applies modifier to each individual particle's lifetime
- Parameters: type, timeStart, timeEnd, property, operation, valueStart, valueEnd
- Each particle's modifier runs from its creation time

`addglobalmodifier(str, float, float, str, str, float, float)` - returns object
- Applies modifier globally across all particles based on emitter time
- Parameters: type, timeStart, timeEnd, property, operation, valueStart, valueEnd
- All particles share the same global time reference

`addemitmodifier(str, float, float, str, str, float, float)` - returns object
- Applies modifier at emission time (when particle is created)
- Parameters: type, timeStart, timeEnd, property, operation, valueStart, valueEnd
- Applied once per particle when emitted

**Modifier Type Parameter (first string):**
- `"once"` - Apply once at timeStart
- `"range"` - Apply continuously from timeStart to timeEnd
- `"loop"` - Apply in a repeating loop

**Modifier Operation Parameter (fourth string):**
- `"replace"` - Replace value, interpolating from valueStart to valueEnd
- `"add"` - Add random value between valueStart and valueEnd
- `"multiply"` - Multiply by value (usually valueStart)

**Modifiable Properties (third string):**
- `"x"`, `"y"`, `"z"` - Position
- `"alpha"` - Transparency (0.0-1.0)
- `"red"`, `"green"`, `"blue"` - Color components (0.0-1.0)
- `"zoom"` - Size multiplier
- `"rotation"` - Rotation angle (radians)
- `"angle"` - Movement direction angle (radians)
- `"zangle"` - Z-axis angle (radians)
- `"speed"` - Movement speed
- `"spin"` - Rotation speed (radians per second)

**Modifier Examples:**

```gs2
// Fade out particles over their lifetime (local modifier)
emitter.addlocalmodifier("range", 0, 2, "alpha", "replace", 1, 0);
// Particles start at alpha 1.0 and fade to 0.0 over 2 seconds

// Add random movement variation at emission (emit modifier)
emitter.addemitmodifier("once", 0, 0, "angle", "add", -pi/4, pi/4);
// Each particle gets a random angle between -45° and +45° when created

// Scale particles over time (local modifier)
emitter.addlocalmodifier("range", 0, 2, "zoom", "replace", 0.5, 1.5);
// Particles grow from 0.5x to 1.5x size over 2 seconds

// Color transition (local modifier)
emitter.addlocalmodifier("range", 0, 2, "red", "replace", 1, 0);
emitter.addlocalmodifier("range", 0, 2, "green", "replace", 0, 1);
// Particles transition from red to green over 2 seconds

// Global rotation effect (global modifier)
emitter.addglobalmodifier("loop", 0, 5, "rotation", "replace", 0, 2*pi);
// All particles rotate together in a 5-second loop

// Pulsing effect (global modifier)
emitter.addglobalmodifier("range", 0, 1, "zoom", "replace", 1, 1.5);
emitter.addglobalmodifier("range", 1, 2, "zoom", "replace", 1.5, 1);
// All particles pulse together
```

**Additional Emitter Methods:**

`removemodifiers()` - Remove all modifiers from emitter
`removeparticles()` - Remove all current particles
`emit()` - Force immediate particle emission
`emitat(str)` - Emit particles at position string "{x, y, z}"
`advancetime(float)` - Manually advance particle simulation time

**Complete Emitter Properties Reference:**

**Emitter Configuration:**
- `attachposition` - boolean - Move all particles with image position
- `delaymin` - float - Minimum delay between emissions (seconds)
- `delaymax` - float - Maximum delay between emissions (seconds)
- `emissionoffset` - string - "{x, y, z}" offset from image position
- `firstinfront` - boolean - First particle draws in front of others
- `nrofparticles` - integer - Particles emitted per emission cycle
- `maxparticles` - integer - Maximum particles alive at once
- `emitautomatically` - boolean - Auto-emit based on delays (default: true)
- `autorotation` - boolean - Automatically rotate particles
- `movementfactor` - float - Global movement speed multiplier

**Emission Control:**
- `emitatterrainheight` - boolean - Emit at terrain height
- `forceaboveterrain` - boolean - Force particles above terrain
- `checkbelowterrain` - boolean - Check if particles go below terrain

**Clipping and Rendering:**
- `cliptoscreen` - boolean - Clip particles to screen bounds
- `noclipping` - boolean - Disable all clipping (screen and clipping box)
- `clippingbox` - string - "{x1, y1, x2, y2}" clipping rectangle
- `wraptoclippingbox` - boolean - Wrap particles at clipping box edges
- `showonground` - boolean - Show particles parallel to ground (3D only)
- `showontop` - boolean - Show particles on top of 3D objects (3D only)
- `switchyandzaxis` - boolean - Switch Y and Z axis for 3D rendering

**Emitter State:**
- `isfrozen` - boolean - Freeze particle simulation
- `continueafterdestroy` - boolean - Continue emitting after image destroyed
- `currentparticlecount` - integer (read-only) - Current number of alive particles
- `emittedparticles` - integer (read-only) - Total particles emitted
- `particletypes` - integer - Number of particle types/variations

**Emitter Objects:**
- `particle` - object (read-only) - Base particle properties template
- `dropemitter` - object (read-only) - Emitter for particle drops
- `dropwateremitter` - object (read-only) - Emitter for water drops

#### Emitter Examples

##### Fire Effect
//#CLIENTSIDE
function createFireEffect(x, y) {
  showimg(100, "", x, y);
  with (findimg(100)) {
    layer = 1;
    emitter.attachposition = true;
    emitter.delaymin = 0.05;
    emitter.delaymax = 0.15;
    emitter.emissionoffset = "{0, 0, 0}";
    emitter.nrofparticles = 2;
    emitter.maxparticles = 30;
    
    emitter.particle.lifetime = 1.5;
    emitter.particle.image = "fire_particle.png";
    emitter.particle.mode = 0;
    emitter.particle.alpha = 0.9;
    emitter.particle.zoom = 0.8;
    emitter.particle.red = 1.0;
    emitter.particle.green = 0.3;
    emitter.particle.blue = 0.0;
    emitter.particle.zangle = pi/2; // Upward
    emitter.particle.angle = 0;
    emitter.particle.speed = 1.5;
    emitter.particle.spin = 0.5;
    
    // Fade out and move upward
    emitter.addLocalModifier("range", 0, 1.5, "alpha", "replace", 1, 0);
    emitter.addLocalModifier("range", 0, 1.5, "y", "add", -0.5, -1.5);
    emitter.addLocalModifier("range", 0, 1.5, "zoom", "replace", 0.8, 0.2);
  }
}

##### Magic Sparkles
//#CLIENTSIDE
function createMagicSparkles(x, y) {
  showimg(101, "", x, y);
  with (findimg(101)) {
    layer = 2;
    emitter.attachposition = true;
    emitter.delaymin = 0.1;
    emitter.delaymax = 0.3;
    emitter.emissionoffset = "{-1, -1, 0}";
    emitter.nrofparticles = 3;
    emitter.maxparticles = 20;
    
    emitter.particle.lifetime = 2.0;
    emitter.particle.image = "sparkle.png";
    emitter.particle.mode = 0;
    emitter.particle.alpha = 0.8;
    emitter.particle.zoom = 0.5;
    emitter.particle.red = 0.5;
    emitter.particle.green = 0.8;
    emitter.particle.blue = 1.0;
    emitter.particle.zangle = 0;
    emitter.particle.angle = 0;
    emitter.particle.speed = 1.0;
    emitter.particle.spin = 2.0;
    
    // Random movement and fade
    emitter.addLocalModifier("once", 0, 0, "angle", "add", 0, 2*pi);
    emitter.addLocalModifier("range", 0, 2, "alpha", "replace", 1, 0);
    emitter.addLocalModifier("range", 0, 2, "zoom", "replace", 0.5, 0.1);
  }
}

##### One-time Explosion
//#CLIENTSIDE
function createExplosion(x, y) {
  showimg(102, "", x, y);
  with (findimg(102)) {
    layer = 1;
    emitter.attachposition = false;
    emitter.delaymin = 10000; // Very long delay
    emitter.delaymax = 10000;
    emitter.emissionoffset = "{0, 0, 0}";
    emitter.nrofparticles = 15;
    emitter.maxparticles = 15;
    
    emitter.particle.lifetime = 1.0;
    emitter.particle.image = "explosion_particle.png";
    emitter.particle.mode = 0;
    emitter.particle.alpha = 1.0;
    emitter.particle.zoom = 1.0;
    emitter.particle.red = 1.0;
    emitter.particle.green = 0.5;
    emitter.particle.blue = 0.0;
    emitter.particle.zangle = 0;
    emitter.particle.angle = 0;
    emitter.particle.speed = 3.0;
    emitter.particle.spin = 1.0;
    
    // Random directions
    emitter.addLocalModifier("once", 0, 0, "angle", "add", 0, 2*pi);
    emitter.addLocalModifier("range", 0, 1, "alpha", "replace", 1, 0);
    emitter.addLocalModifier("range", 0, 1, "zoom", "replace", 1, 0.2);
    
    // Force emission
    emitter.emit();
    
    // Hide after explosion
    scheduleEvent(2, "hideExplosion");
  }
}

function onHideExplosion() {
  hideimg(102);
}

#### Emitter Properties Reference

**Emitter Configuration:**
- `emitter.attachposition` - Boolean: Move all particles with image
- `emitter.delaymin` - Float: Minimum delay between emissions (seconds)
- `emitter.delaymax` - Float: Maximum delay between emissions (seconds)
- `emitter.emissionoffset` - String: "{x, y, z}" offset from image position
- `emitter.firstinfront` - Boolean: First particle draws in front
- `emitter.nrofparticles` - Integer: Particles emitted per cycle
- `emitter.maxparticles` - Integer: Maximum particles at once

**Particle Properties:**
- `emitter.particle.lifetime` - Float: How long particles exist (seconds)
- `emitter.particle.image` - String: Image file for particles
- `emitter.particle.mode` - Integer: 0=add, 1=trans, 2=subtract
- `emitter.particle.alpha` - Float: Transparency (0-1)
- `emitter.particle.zoom` - Float: Size multiplier
- `emitter.particle.red` - Float: Red color component (0-1)
- `emitter.particle.green` - Float: Green color component (0-1)
- `emitter.particle.blue` - Float: Blue color component (0-1)
- `emitter.particle.zangle` - Float: Upward angle (0 to 2*pi)
- `emitter.particle.angle` - Float: Direction angle (0 to 2*pi)
- `emitter.particle.speed` - Float: Movement speed
- `emitter.particle.spin` - Float: Rotations during lifetime
- `emitter.particle.stretchx` - Float: Width stretch factor
- `emitter.particle.stretchy` - Float: Height stretch factor

**Modifier Functions:**
- `emitter.addLocalModifier(selection_type, range_min, range_max, modified_attrib, process, new_min, new_max)`
- `emitter.emit()` - Force immediate emission

**Selection Types:**
- `"once"` - Modify particles once at creation
- `"range"` - Modify particles continuously within lifetime range

**Process Types:**
- `"replace"` - Fade from new_min to new_max over range
- `"add"` - Add random value between new_min and new_max
- `"multiply"` - Multiply by random value between new_min and new_max

### GS2 Advanced GUI System

#### GUI Control Creation and Management
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  // Create main window
  this.mainWindow = new GuiWindowCtrl("MainWindow") {
    profile = "GuiWindowProfile";
    x = 50;
    y = 50;
    width = 300;
    height = 200;
    text = "My Window";
    canClose = true;
    canMove = true;
    canResize = true;
  };
  
  // Create button
  this.button = new GuiButtonCtrl("MyButton") {
    profile = "GuiButtonProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 30;
    width = 100;
    height = 25;
    text = "Click Me";
  };
  
  // Create text control
  this.textCtrl = new GuiTextCtrl("MyText") {
    profile = "GuiTextProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 60;
    width = 200;
    height = 20;
    text = "Hello World";
    font = "Arial";
    fontSize = 12;
    fontColor = "255 255 255";
  };
  
  // Create text edit control
  this.textEdit = new GuiTextEditCtrl("MyTextEdit") {
    profile = "GuiTextEditProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 90;
    width = 200;
    height = 20;
    text = "";
    maxChars = 50;
  };
  
  // Add controls to window
  this.mainWindow.add(this.button);
  this.mainWindow.add(this.textCtrl);
  this.mainWindow.add(this.textEdit);
  
  // Add window to canvas
  Canvas.add(this.mainWindow);
}

// Button click event
function onMyButtonClick() {
  this.textCtrl.text = "Button clicked!";
  this.textEdit.text = "You clicked the button!";
}

// Text edit change event
function onMyTextEditChange() {
  this.textCtrl.text = "You typed: " @ this.textEdit.text;
}

#### Advanced GUI Examples

##### Inventory System
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  this.inventoryWindow = new GuiWindowCtrl("InventoryWindow") {
    profile = "GuiWindowProfile";
    x = 100;
    y = 100;
    width = 400;
    height = 300;
    text = "Inventory";
    canClose = true;
    canMove = true;
    visible = false;
  };
  
  this.inventoryList = new GuiTextListCtrl("InventoryList") {
    profile = "GuiTextListProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 30;
    width = 200;
    height = 200;
    columns = 2;
    column[0] = "Item";
    column[1] = "Count";
  };
  
  this.itemInfo = new GuiMLTextCtrl("ItemInfo") {
    profile = "GuiMLTextProfile";
    x = 220;
    y = 30;
    width = 170;
    height = 200;
    text = "Select an item to view details.";
  };
  
  this.useButton = new GuiButtonCtrl("UseButton") {
    profile = "GuiButtonProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 240;
    width = 80;
    height = 25;
    text = "Use";
    enabled = false;
  };
  
  this.dropButton = new GuiButtonCtrl("DropButton") {
    profile = "GuiButtonProfile";
    x = 100;
    y = 240;
    width = 80;
    height = 25;
    text = "Drop";
    enabled = false;
  };
  
  this.inventoryWindow.add(this.inventoryList);
  this.inventoryWindow.add(this.itemInfo);
  this.inventoryWindow.add(this.useButton);
  this.inventoryWindow.add(this.dropButton);
  
  Canvas.add(this.inventoryWindow);
  
  // Load inventory data
  this.loadInventory();
}

function loadInventory() {
  this.inventoryList.clear();
  
  // Add sample items
  this.inventoryList.addRow(0, "Sword", "1");
  this.inventoryList.addRow(1, "Shield", "1");
  this.inventoryList.addRow(2, "Health Potion", "5");
  this.inventoryList.addRow(3, "Mana Potion", "3");
  this.inventoryList.addRow(4, "Gold Coin", "100");
}

function onInventoryListSelect() {
  temp.selectedId = this.inventoryList.selectedId;
  temp.itemName = this.inventoryList.getRowText(selectedId, 0);
  temp.itemCount = this.inventoryList.getRowText(selectedId, 1);
  
  // Update item info
  this.itemInfo.text = "Item: " @ itemName @ "\n";
  this.itemInfo.text @= "Count: " @ itemCount @ "\n\n";
  
  if (itemName == "Sword") {
    this.itemInfo.text @= "A sharp blade that deals damage to enemies.";
  } else if (itemName == "Shield") {
    this.itemInfo.text @= "Protects you from incoming attacks.";
  } else if (itemName == "Health Potion") {
    this.itemInfo.text @= "Restores health when consumed.";
  } else if (itemName == "Mana Potion") {
    this.itemInfo.text @= "Restores mana when consumed.";
  } else if (itemName == "Gold Coin") {
    this.itemInfo.text @= "Currency used for trading.";
  }
  
  // Enable buttons
  this.useButton.enabled = true;
  this.dropButton.enabled = true;
}

function onUseButtonClick() {
  temp.selectedId = this.inventoryList.selectedId;
  temp.itemName = this.inventoryList.getRowText(selectedId, 0);
  
  if (itemName == "Health Potion") {
    // Use health potion
    triggerServer("gui", this.name, "useItem", "Health Potion");
    this.loadInventory(); // Refresh inventory
  } else if (itemName == "Mana Potion") {
    // Use mana potion
    triggerServer("gui", this.name, "useItem", "Mana Potion");
    this.loadInventory(); // Refresh inventory
  }
}

function onDropButtonClick() {
  temp.selectedId = this.inventoryList.selectedId;
  temp.itemName = this.inventoryList.getRowText(selectedId, 0);
  
  // Drop item
  triggerServer("gui", this.name, "dropItem", itemName);
  this.loadInventory(); // Refresh inventory
}

// Toggle inventory window
function toggleInventory() {
  this.inventoryWindow.visible = !this.inventoryWindow.visible;
}

##### Chat System
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  this.chatWindow = new GuiWindowCtrl("ChatWindow") {
    profile = "GuiWindowProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 10;
    width = 400;
    height = 300;
    text = "Chat";
    canClose = false;
    canMove = true;
    canResize = true;
  };
  
  this.chatDisplay = new GuiMLTextCtrl("ChatDisplay") {
    profile = "GuiMLTextProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 30;
    width = 380;
    height = 200;
    text = "Welcome to the chat!\n";
    hScrollBar = "alwaysOff";
    vScrollBar = "alwaysOn";
  };
  
  this.chatInput = new GuiTextEditCtrl("ChatInput") {
    profile = "GuiTextEditProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 240;
    width = 300;
    height = 20;
    text = "";
    maxChars = 200;
  };
  
  this.sendButton = new GuiButtonCtrl("SendButton") {
    profile = "GuiButtonProfile";
    x = 320;
    y = 240;
    width = 70;
    height = 20;
    text = "Send";
  };
  
  this.chatWindow.add(this.chatDisplay);
  this.chatWindow.add(this.chatInput);
  this.chatWindow.add(this.sendButton);
  
  Canvas.add(this.chatWindow);
}

function onChatInputEnter() {
  this.sendMessage();
}

function onSendButtonClick() {
  this.sendMessage();
}

function sendMessage() {
  temp.message = this.chatInput.text;
  if (message.length() > 0) {
    // Send to server
    triggerServer("gui", this.name, "sendChat", message);
    
    // Add to local display
    this.addChatMessage(player.nick, message);
    
    // Clear input
    this.chatInput.text = "";
  }
}

function addChatMessage(sender, message) {
  temp.timestamp = formatTimeString("%H:%M", timevar2);
  this.chatDisplay.text @= "[" @ timestamp @ "] " @ sender @ ": " @ message @ "\n";
  
  // Auto-scroll to bottom
  this.chatDisplay.scrollToBottom();
}

// Server-side chat handler
function onActionServerside(action, data) {
  if (action == "sendChat") {
    // Broadcast to all players
    sendToNC("chat", player.nick, data);
  } else if (action == "chat") {
    // Add received message to display
    this.addChatMessage(data[0], data[1]);
  }
}
