#### TProjectile Object Properties and Methods

Projectiles are created by `shoot()`, `shootarrow()`, `shootfireball()`, `shootfireblast()`, `shootnuke()`, `shootball()`, etc. They have properties and events:

**Basic Properties:**
- `projectile.x` - float - X position
- `projectile.y` - float - Y position
- `projectile.z` - float - Z height
- `projectile.dir` - int - Direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)
- `projectile.power` - float - Hit power
- `projectile.img` - string - Image name
- `projectile.ani` - string - Animation name
- `projectile.owner` - TServerPlayer - Player who shot projectile (if any)

**Movement:**
- `projectile.speed` - float - Movement speed
- `projectile.vx` - float - X velocity
- `projectile.vy` - float - Y velocity
- `projectile.vz` - float - Z velocity (for arc projectiles)

**Lifecycle:**
- `projectile.lifetime` - float - Time until projectile expires
- `projectile.hitwall` - boolean - Did projectile hit a wall
- `projectile.hitplayer` - TServerPlayer - Player that was hit (if any)
- `projectile.hitnpc` - TServerNPC - NPC that was hit (if any)

**Projectile Events (in weapon script):**
```gs2
// In weapon script that created projectile
function onActionProjectile2(projectile) {
  // Called when projectile hits something
  if (projectile.hitplayer != null) {
    projectile.hitplayer.chat = "You were hit by my projectile!";
    hitplayer(projectile.hitplayer, projectile.power, projectile.x, projectile.y);
  }
  if (projectile.hitnpc != null) {
    hitnpc(projectile.hitnpc, projectile.power, projectile.x, projectile.y);
  }
  if (projectile.hitwall) {
    // Projectile hit a wall
  }
}

function onActionProjectileTimeout(projectile) {
  // Called when projectile expires without hitting anything
}
```

**Projectile Methods:**
- `projectile.destroy()` - Remove projectile immediately

---

#### TGraalVar Base Object Type

Most objects in GS2 inherit from `TGraalVar`, which provides fundamental variable and array operations:

**Basic Properties:**
- All objects support dynamic properties (can add new properties at runtime)
- Objects can be converted to strings, numbers, or arrays

**Object Type Detection:**
- `objecttype()` - returns string - Get object type name (e.g., "TGraalVar", "TServerPlayer", "TServerNPC", "TProjectile")

**Variable Operations:**
- `copyfrom(obj)` - Copy all variables and array values from another object
- Objects can have nested properties (e.g., `player.inventory[0].itemname`)

**Array Operations (all objects support these):**
- `array.add(obj)` - Add element to array
- `array.remove(obj)` - Remove element from array
- `array.size()` - Get array size
- `array.clear()` - Clear all elements
- `array.delete(int)` - Delete element at index
- `array.insert(int, obj)` - Insert element at index
- `array.replace(int, obj)` - Replace element at index
- `array.index(obj)` - Find index of element
- `array.indices(obj)` - Find all indices of element
- `array.subarray(int, int)` - Extract subarray

**String Operations:**
- `savelines(str, int)` - Save array to file
- `loadlines(str)` - Load file into array
- `savestring(str, int)` - Save variable as string to file
- `loadstring(str)` - Load file as string
- `savevarstoarray(bool)` - Save subvars to array format
- `loadvarsfromarray(obj)` - Load variables from array format

**XML/JSON Operations:**
- `loadxml(str)` - Load XML file into object
- `loadxmlfromstring(str)` - Load XML string into object
- `savexml(str, int)` - Save object to XML file
- `savexmltostring()` - Save object to XML string
- `savejsontostring(int)` - Save object to JSON string

**Array Manipulation:**
- `sortascending()` - Sort array ascending
- `sortdescending()` - Sort array descending
- `sortbyvalue(str, str, bool)` - Sort by member variable
- `addarray(obj)` - Add another array
- `addarraymember(str)` - Add array member by name
- `getarraymember(str)` - Get array member by name
- `insertarray(int, obj)` - Insert array at index
- `subarray(int, int)` - Extract subarray
- `subarray2(int, int, int, int)` - Extract 2D subarray

**Class System:**
- `join(str)` - Join a class (inherit functions/events)
- `joinedclasses` - Object with joined classes
- `isinclass(str)` - Check if in class, returns boolean
- `leave(str)` - Leave previously joined class

#### TClientPlayer Object Properties (Client-side)

On the client-side, `player` is a `TClientPlayer` object with properties similar to `TServerPlayer` but focused on client-side rendering and interaction:

**Basic Information:**
- `player.account` - string - Account name
- `player.nick` - string - Display name
- `player.id` - integer - Player ID
- `player.x` - float - X position
- `player.y` - float - Y position
- `player.z` - float - Z height

**Appearance:**
- `player.head` - string - Head image
- `player.body` - string - Body image
- `player.sword` - string - Sword image
- `player.shield` - string - Shield image
- `player.ani` - string - Current animation
- `player.dir` - int - Direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)
- `player.visible` - boolean - Is player visible

**Stats (Read-only on client):**
- `player.hearts` - float - Hearts
- `player.rupees` - integer - Rupees
- `player.bombs` - integer - Bombs
- `player.arrows` - integer - Arrows
- `player.darts` - integer - Darts

**Movement:**
- `player.moving` - boolean - Is player moving
- `player.carrying` - boolean - Is carrying something

**Client-side Methods:**
- `player.chat = "text"` - Set player's chat text (visual only on client)
- Client-side player object is read-only for most properties (set on server)

---

#### TLevel Object Properties (Client-side)

The `level` object on the client provides information about the current level:

**Basic Properties:**
- `level.name` - string - Level name
- `level.width` - integer - Level width in tiles
- `level.height` - integer - Level height in tiles
- `level.board` - string - Board name

**Position:**
- `level.x` - float - Camera X position
- `level.y` - float - Camera Y position
- `level.zoom` - float - Zoom level

**Client-side Methods:**
- `level.gettile(int, int)` - Get tile type at coordinates, returns integer
- `level.gettilex(float)` - Convert world X to tile X, returns integer
- `level.gettiley(float)` - Convert world Y to tile Y, returns integer
- `level.getworldx(int)` - Convert tile X to world X, returns float
- `level.getworldy(int)` - Convert tile Y to world Y, returns float

---

#### TGraalImage Object Properties

Images loaded with `loadimg()` return a `TGraalImage` object:

**Basic Properties:**
- `image.width` - integer (read-only) - Image width in pixels
- `image.height` - integer (read-only) - Image height in pixels
- `image.filename` - string (read-only) - Image filename
- `image.format` - string (read-only) - Image format (PNG, GIF, etc.)

**Methods:**
- `image.getpixel(int, int)` - Get pixel color at coordinates, returns string "{r, g, b, a}"
- `image.setpixel(int, int, string)` - Set pixel color at coordinates, params: x, y, "{r, g, b, a}"
- `image.copy(int, int, int, int)` - Copy rectangle from image, returns new image object
- `image.resize(int, int)` - Resize image, returns new image object
- `image.save(string)` - Save image to file

**Example:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
temp.img = loadimg("sprite.png");
temp.width = img.width;
temp.height = img.height;
temp.pixel = img.getpixel(10, 10); // "{255, 128, 64, 255}"
img.setpixel(10, 10, "{128, 128, 128, 255}"); // Set to gray
temp.resized = img.resize(64, 64);
resized.save("resized.png");
```

---

#### Network and Synchronization

**Server-Client Synchronization:**
- Server-side variables are automatically synchronized to clients
- Use `triggerclient()` to send data from server to client
- Use `triggerserver()` to send data from client to server
- Arrays are synchronized automatically (modifications propagate)

**Variable Synchronization:**
```gs2
// Server-side
function onCreated() {
  this.myvar = "Hello"; // Automatically synced to clients
  this.myarray = {"a", "b", "c"}; // Array synced automatically
}

// Client-side receives automatically synced variables
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onPlayerEnters() {
  echo(myvar); // Outputs "Hello"
  echo(myarray.size()); // Outputs 3
}
```

**Manual Synchronization:**
```gs2
// Server-side
function updatePlayer() {
  triggerclient("gui", this.name, "updateStatus", player.name, player.hearts);
}

// Client-side
//#CLIENTSIDE
function updateStatus(playerName, hearts) {
  echo("Player " @ playerName @ " has " @ hearts @ " hearts");
}
```

**Rate Limiting:**
- Server can throttle client triggers to prevent spam
- Client triggers are rate-limited to prevent abuse
- Use timers to control update frequency

---

#### Advanced Class System

**GS2 Class System (using join()):**
```gs2
// GS2 doesn't have ES6 class syntax - classes are separate script files that you join
// Create a class script file: "BaseNPC.nw"
function onCreated() {
  this.health = 100;
  this.maxhealth = 100;
}

function takeDamage(amount) {
  this.health -= amount;
  if (this.health <= 0) {
    this.die();
  }
}

function die() {
  // Override in subclasses
}

// Create another class: "Weapon.nw"
function onCreated() {
  this.ammo = 10;
}

function shoot() {
  if (this.ammo > 0) {
    shootarrow(1, player.x, player.y, player.dir);
    this.ammo--;
  }
}

function die() {
  echo("Weapon destroyed");
}

// Use in your NPC script
function onCreated() {
  this.join("Weapon"); // Join the Weapon class to inherit its functions
  this.health = 150; // Override default
}
```

**Dynamic Class Joining:**
```gs2
function onCreated() {
  if (player.guild == "Warriors") {
    this.join("WarriorNPC");
  } else if (player.guild == "Mages") {
    this.join("MageNPC");
  }
}

// Classes can be joined/unjoined dynamically
function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  if (this.isinclass("WarriorNPC")) {
    this.leave("WarriorNPC");
    this.join("EnemyNPC");
  }
}
```

**Class Events:**
- Events in joined classes are called on the object
- Class functions can be overridden
- `this` context refers to the object, not the class

```gs2
temp.healthies = {};
for (temp.pl : players) {
  if (pl.hearts > 3) {
    healthies.add(pl);  // Use .add() method instead of index assignment for empty arrays
  }
}

// Array copying
temp.copy = {};
temp.copy.copyfrom(healthies);

// Array sorting by value
temp.items = {
  "Sword": 100,

  "Potion": 25

```

```gs2
temp.row = map[0]; // {1, 2, 3}
temp.cell = map[0][1]; // 2
temp.sub = map.subarray2(0, 0, 2, 2); // {{1, 2}, {4, 5}}
```

**Array Searching:**

```gs2
temp.exists = (array.index("e") != null); // false
```

#### Database Operations and SQL Queries

**Basic SQL Query:**
```gs2
// requestsql() - Execute SQL query (synchronous)
temp.result = requestsql("SELECT * FROM players WHERE account = ?", player.account);
if (result.size() > 0) {
  temp.playerData = result[0];
  echo("Player found: " @ playerData.account);
}

// requestsql2() - Execute SQL query with callback (asynchronous)
function onSQLResponse(result) {
  if (result.size() > 0) {
    temp.playerData = result[0];
    echo("Player data loaded: " @ playerData.account);
  }
}
requestsql2("SELECT * FROM players WHERE account = ?", player.account);

// requestsql2 with callback function
requestsql2("SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM players", "", "onPlayerCount");
function onPlayerCount(result) {
  if (result.size() > 0) {
    echo("Total players: " @ result[0].count);
  }
}
```

**SQL Query Patterns:**
```gs2
// Parameterized queries (prevent SQL injection)
temp.account = "SomePlayer";
temp.result = requestsql("SELECT * FROM players WHERE account = ?", account);

// Multiple parameters
temp.result = requestsql("SELECT * FROM players WHERE account = ? AND guild = ?", account, "Warriors");

// INSERT query
temp.newId = requestsql("INSERT INTO items (name, price) VALUES (?, ?)", "Sword", 100);
if (newId.size() > 0) {
  echo("Inserted with ID: " @ newId[0].id);
}

// UPDATE query
temp.updated = requestsql("UPDATE players SET rupees = ? WHERE account = ?", 500, account);

// DELETE query
temp.deleted = requestsql("DELETE FROM items WHERE id = ?", itemId);
```

**SQL Result Object:**
- `result.size()` - Number of rows returned
- `result[index]` - Access row by index (0-based)
- `result[index].columnname` - Access column value
- `result[0].id` - Common pattern for single-row results

**Database Best Practices:**
- Always use parameterized queries (?) to prevent SQL injection
- Use `requestsql2()` for non-blocking queries
- Handle empty results gracefully
- Index commonly queried columns for performance

#### HTTP Requests and Web Integration

**Basic HTTP GET:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
function downloadData() {
  downloadurltoarray("http://example.com/api/data", "", "onDataReceived");
}

function onDataReceived(result) {
  echo("Data received: " @ result.size() @ " lines");
  for (temp.line : result) {
    echo(line);
  }
}
```

**HTTP POST with Data:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
function sendData() {
  temp.data = {"key": "value", "number": 42};
  temp.jsonData = data.savejsontostring(0);
  downloadurltoarray("http://example.com/api/endpoint", jsonData, "onResponse");
}
```

**URL Functions:**
- `downloadurltoarray(url, postdata, callback)` - Download URL to array, triggers callback function
- `downloadurltofile(url, filename, postdata, callback)` - Download URL to file
- `uploadfile(filename, url, callback)` - Upload file to URL
- `downloadfile(url, filename, callback)` - Download file from URL

**Error Handling:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
function downloadData() {
  downloadurltoarray("http://example.com/api/data", "", "onDataReceived");
  settimer(5.0); // Timeout after 5 seconds
}

function onTimeout() {
  echo("Download timeout");
}

function onDataReceived(result) {
  cancelevents(); // Cancel timeout
  if (result != null && result.size() > 0) {
    echo("Data received successfully");
  } else {
    echo("No data received");
  }
}
```

#### Advanced Animation System

**Animation Files (GANI):**
- GANI files define sprite sheet animations
- Store frame sequences, timing, and directional variants
- Loaded automatically when referenced

**Setting Animations:**
```gs2
// NPC animation
setcharani("walk", ""); // Play walk animation
setcharani("sword_attack", "power=5"); // Play with parameters

// Player animation
player.ani = "walk"; // Set player animation

// Image animation
temp.img = showimg(1, "sprite.png", 10, 10);
img.ani = "idle"; // Play animation on image
```

**Animation Parameters:**
```gs2
// Animation parameters modify animation behavior
setcharani("walk", "speed=2.0"); // Double speed
setcharani("attack", "loop=false"); // Don't loop
setcharani("spell", "reverse=true"); // Play in reverse

// Get current animation
temp.currentAni = player.ani;
```

**Animation Events:**
```gs2
// Animation finished event (may be server-specific - verify with your server)
// Note: onAniFinished() may not exist in standard GS2 - check your server's documentation
function onAniFinished() {
  if (player.ani == "sword_attack") {
    player.ani = "idle"; // Return to idle after attack
  }
}

// Animation can trigger events
function onCreated() {
  this.attackAnimation = false;
}

function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  setcharani("attack", "");
  this.attackAnimation = true;
}

// Animation finished event (may be server-specific - verify with your server)
function onAniFinished() {
  if (this.attackAnimation) {
    hitplayer(player, 5, this.x, this.y);
    this.attackAnimation = false;
    setcharani("idle", "");
  }
}
```

#### Image Part System (Sprite Sheets)

**Basic Image Parts:**
```gs2
// Set image part (sprite sheet coordinates)
setimgpart("sprite.png", 0, 0, 32, 32); // Show first 32x32 sprite
setimgpart("sprite.png", 32, 0, 32, 32); // Show second sprite in row

// Image part for NPCs
setimg("sprite.png");
setimgpart("sprite.png", 0, 0, 32, 32); // Define which part to show
```

**Clientside Image Parts:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
temp.img = showimg(1, "spritesheet.png", 10, 10);
img.partx = 0;   // X offset in sprite sheet
img.party = 0;   // Y offset in sprite sheet
img.partw = 32;  // Width of sprite to show
img.parth = 32;  // Height of sprite to show

// Change sprite frame
function animateSprite() {
  temp.img = findimg(1);
  if (img != null) {
    img.partx = (img.partx + 32) % 128; // Move to next frame
    if (img.partx == 0) {
      img.party = (img.party + 32) % 128; // Move to next row
    }
  }
  scheduleevent(0.1, "animateSprite");
}
```

**Sprite Sheet Animation:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  this.frame = 0;
  this.maxFrames = 4;
  this.frameTime = 0.1;
  this.spriteWidth = 32;
  this.spriteHeight = 32;
  
  temp.img = showimg(1, "sprite_sheet.png", player.x, player.y);
  img.layer = 1;
  scheduleevent(frameTime, "animate");
}

function animate() {
  temp.img = findimg(1);
  if (img != null) {
    img.partx = frame * spriteWidth;
    img.party = 0;
    img.partw = spriteWidth;
    img.parth = spriteHeight;
    
    frame++;
    if (frame >= maxFrames) {
      frame = 0;
    }
    
    scheduleevent(frameTime, "animate");
  }
}
```

**Image Part Utilities:**
```gs2
// changeimgpart() - Change image part for existing image
changeimgpart(1, 64, 0, 32, 32); // Change image 1 to show sprite at (64, 0)

// Useful for tile-based sprites
```

#### Advanced Triggers and Communication

**triggerclient() - Server to Client:**
```gs2
// Server-side: Trigger client GUI script
function onCreated() {
  triggerclient("gui", player.account, "updateUI", player.hearts, player.rupees);
}

// Client-side GUI script
//#CLIENTSIDE
function updateUI(hearts, rupees) {
  echo("Hearts: " @ hearts @ ", Rupees: " @ rupees);
}

// Server-side: Trigger client weapon script
function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  triggerclient("weapon", player.account, "addWeaponPower", 5);
}

// Client-side weapon script
//#CLIENTSIDE
function addWeaponPower(amount) {
  this.weaponPower += amount;
  echo("Weapon power increased!");
}
```

**triggerclient Variants:**
- `triggerclient("gui", target, function, ...)` - Trigger client GUI script (target is player account)
- `triggerclient("weapon", target, function, ...)` - Trigger client weapon script (target is player account)

**triggerserver() - Client to Server:**
```gs2
// Client-side: Send to NPC database
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  triggerserver("npc", "ShopDB", "buyItem", player.account, "sword");
}

// Server-side NPC database (name must match "ShopDB")
function buyItem(account, itemName) {
  temp.pl = findplayer(account);
  if (pl != null) {
    chat = "You bought a " @ itemName;
  }
}

// Client-side: Send to weapon script
//#CLIENTSIDE
function requestWeaponUpgrade() {
  triggerserver("weapon", this.name, "upgradeWeapon", player.account);
}

// Server-side weapon script (name must match target)
function upgradeWeapon(account) {
  temp.pl = findplayer(account);
  if (pl != null) {
    triggerclient("weapon", account, "weaponUpgraded", true);
  }
}

// Client-side GUI to NPC
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  triggerserver("gui", this.name, "clientReady", player.account);
}

// Server-side NPC (name must match target)
function clientReady(account) {
  echo("Client " @ account @ " is ready");
  triggerclient("gui", account, "welcome", "Welcome to the server!");
}
```

**Level-wide Communication (using triggeraction):**
```gs2
// Broadcast to all objects in level using triggeraction
function broadcastToLevel(eventName, data) {
  for (temp.pl : findareaplayers(0, 0, level.width, level.height)) {
    triggeraction(pl.x, pl.y, eventName, data);
  }
}

// Objects receive via onAction{EventName} handler
function onActionUpdateGameState(params) {
  temp.parts = params.split(",");
  this.gameTime = strtofloat(parts[0]);
  this.score = strtofloat(parts[1]);
}
```

**triggeraction() - Tile-based Triggers:**
```gs2
// Trigger action at specific tile coordinates
// Actions trigger specific event handlers based on action name
triggeraction(player.x, player.y, "ChestOpen", "");

// NPCs/weapons at that position receive the action via specific event handler:
// For action "ChestOpen", the handler is onActionChestOpen()
function onActionChestOpen() {
  // Handle chest opening
  this.open();
}

// Alternative: Actions can also be checked with flags
// For action "ChestOpen", the flag is actionChestOpen
if (actionChestOpen) {
  this.open();
}

// Common pattern: triggeraction is often used with projectile hits
// This triggers onActionCluck() or if (actionCluck) in the shooting weapon
triggeraction(player.x, player.y, "Cluck", {player.dir, player.name});
```

#### Level Editing and Tile Manipulation

**Note:** Most tile manipulation functions (`level.gettile`, `level.settile`, `level.gettilelayer`, `level.settilelayer`, `level.gettiledef`, `level.blocks`, `level.findpath`, `putlevel2`) are not available in standard GS2. Level editing is typically done through the level editor tool or server-specific functions.

**Available Functions:**
```gs2
// Check NPC collision at position (only valid level function found)
temp.collision = level.testnpc(x, y); // Returns integer - checks if NPC blocks position

// Level properties
level.name = "My Level";  // Level name
level.board = "mylevel";  // Level board identifier
level.width = 50;         // Level width in tiles
level.height = 50;        // Level height in tiles
```

**Collision Detection (Alternative Approaches):**
```gs2
// Use findpathinarray for pathfinding instead of level.findpath
// See pathfinding section below for details

// Check position using findpathinarray
// Note: findpathinarray takes (allowed, blocked, stopTiles, noStopTiles, startX, startY, maxLength)
// startX and startY should be integer tile coordinates, not float pixel coordinates
temp.allowed = {0, 1, 2}; // Walkable tiles
temp.blocked = {3, 4};   // Blocked tiles
temp.startTileX = int(this.x / 0.64); // Convert pixel X to tile X (assuming 0.64 tiles per pixel)
temp.startTileY = int(this.y / 0.64); // Convert pixel Y to tile Y
temp.path = findpathinarray(allowed, blocked, {}, {}, startTileX, startTileY, 100);

// Check NPC collision
temp.collision = level.testnpc(x, y); // Returns integer (0 if no collision)
```

#### Advanced NPC Movement and Pathfinding

**Basic Movement:**
```gs2
// Simple movement
move(1, 0, 1.0, 0); // Move right 1 tile in 1 second

// Movement with options
move(2, 2, 2.0, 4 + 8); // Move 2 tiles right, 2 down, with block check and event

// Movement callback
function onMovementFinished() {
  echo("Movement complete at " @ this.x @ ", " @ this.y);
}
```

**Pathfinding (Serverside):**
```gs2
// Simple pathfinding to position
// Note: sqrt() is clientside only - use distance squared for serverside
function moveToTarget(targetX, targetY) {
  temp.dx = targetX - this.x;
  temp.dy = targetY - this.y;
  temp.distanceSq = dx*dx + dy*dy;
  temp.minDistanceSq = 0.25; // 0.5^2
  
  if (distanceSq > minDistanceSq) {
    // Move in direction without sqrt - use fixed step size
    temp.step = 0.5;
    temp.dirX = 0;
    temp.dirY = 0;
    if (dx > 0) dirX = step;
    else if (dx < 0) dirX = -step;
    if (dy > 0) dirY = step;
    else if (dy < 0) dirY = -step;
    move(dirX, dirY, 0.5, 4); // 4 = block check
    scheduleevent(0.5, "moveToTarget", targetX, targetY);
  }
}

// Advanced pathfinding using findpathinarray
function findPathTo(targetX, targetY) {
  temp.allowed = {0, 1, 2}; // Walkable tiles
  temp.blocked = {3, 4}; // Blocked tiles
  // Note: findpathinarray requires integer tile coordinates
  temp.startTileX = int(this.x / 0.64); // Convert pixel X to tile X
  temp.startTileY = int(this.y / 0.64); // Convert pixel Y to tile Y
  temp.path = findpathinarray(allowed, blocked, {}, {}, startTileX, startTileY, 100);
  
  if (path != null && path.size() > 0) {
    // Follow path
    followPath(path);
  }
}
```

**Following Player (Serverside - findnearestplayer is serverside only):**
```gs2
function onCreated() {
  this.followTarget = null;
  this.followDistance = 2.0;
  this.followDistanceSq = followDistance * followDistance; // Store distance squared (sqrt doesn't exist serverside)
  settimer(0.1);
}

function onTimeout() {
  if (followTarget == null) {
    temp.nearest = findnearestplayer(this.x, this.y);
    if (nearest != null) {
      // Use distance squared since sqrt() is clientside only
      temp.dx = nearest.x - this.x;
      temp.dy = nearest.y - this.y;
      temp.distanceSq = dx*dx + dy*dy;
      if (distanceSq > this.followDistanceSq) {
        followTarget = nearest;
      }
    }
  }
  
  if (followTarget != null) {
    temp.dx = followTarget.x - this.x;
    temp.dy = followTarget.y - this.y;
    temp.distanceSq = dx*dx + dy*dy;
    
    if (distanceSq > this.followDistanceSq) {
      // Move towards target - use small fixed step in direction (sqrt doesn't exist serverside)
      // Scale dx/dy to a reasonable movement speed
      temp.step = 0.3;
      temp.dirX = 0;
      temp.dirY = 0;
      if (dx > 0) dirX = step;
      else if (dx < 0) dirX = -step;
      if (dy > 0) dirY = step;
      else if (dy < 0) dirY = -step;
      move(dirX, dirY, 0.3, 4);
    } else {
      followTarget = null; // Reached target
    }
  }
  
  settimer(0.1);
}
```

**Avoiding Obstacles:**
```gs2
function moveWithAvoidance(targetX, targetY) {
  temp.dx = targetX - this.x;
  temp.dy = targetY - this.y;
  // Note: sqrt() is clientside only - for serverside, use distance squared or approximation
  temp.distanceSq = dx*dx + dy*dy;
  temp.distance = sqrt(distanceSq); // Only works clientside, otherwise use approximation
  
  if (distance > 0.5) {
    // Check if direct path is blocked using onwall() or onwall2()
    temp.nextX = this.x + dx/distance * 0.5;
    temp.nextY = this.y + dy/distance * 0.5;
    
    if (onwall(nextX, nextY)) {
      // Try alternative paths using pre-sized arrays
      temp.altPaths = {};
      temp.path1 = new[2];
      path1[0] = dx/distance * 0.3;
      path1[1] = dy/distance * 0.5;
      altPaths.add(path1); // Try going slightly up
      
      temp.path2 = new[2];
      path2[0] = dx/distance * 0.5;
      path2[1] = dy/distance * 0.3;
      altPaths.add(path2); // Try going slightly right
      
      for (temp.path : altPaths) {
        if (!onwall(this.x + path[0], this.y + path[1])) {
          move(path[0], path[1], 0.3, 4);
          return;
        }
      }
    } else {
      // Direct path is clear
      move(dx/distance * 0.5, dy/distance * 0.5, 0.5, 4);
    }
  }
}
```

#### Advanced Projectile Systems

**Custom Projectile Behavior:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
// Weapon script with custom projectile
function onCreated() {
  this.ammo = 10;
  this.projectilePower = 5;
}

function onKeyPressed(keycode) {
  if (keycode == 4 && this.ammo > 0) { // D key
    // shootarrow(int) is clientside only, takes direction only
    this.shootarrow(player.dir);
    this.ammo--;
  }
}

// Note: onActionProjectile2 may not be triggered by shootarrow()
// Action handlers are typically triggered by shootparams or trigger() calls
// Example: shoot(x, y, z, angle, zangle, power, "arrow.gani", "CustomParam")
// Would trigger onActionCustomParam() when the projectile hits
```

**Homing Projectiles:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
// Note: Creating true homing projectiles requires using showani() for visual representation
// and manually updating the position based on target tracking
// This is a simplified example - homing projectiles are complex and server-specific

function shootHomingProjectile(targetX, targetY) {
  // Use showani() for visual projectile (public index typically 1-100)
  // Then track and move it towards target using scheduleevent or timers
  temp.target = findnearestplayer(player.x, player.y);
  if (target != null) {
    // Visual representation using showani
    showani(50, player.x, player.y, player.dir, "arrow.gani");
    // Track and update position - see projectile movement section for details
  }
}

// Note: You cannot modify shoot() projectile properties after creation
// Homing requires custom implementation using showani() or visual effects
```

**Multi-Shot Projectiles:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
function shootMultiArrow(count, spread) {
  temp.baseDir = player.dir * pi / 2; // Convert direction to radians
  temp.angleStep = spread / (count - 1);
  temp.startAngle = baseDir - spread / 2;
  
  for (temp.i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    temp.angle = startAngle + i * angleStep;
    // shootarrow() is clientside only - takes direction only
    // Convert angle back to direction (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)
    temp.dir = int(angle / (pi / 2)) % 4;
    this.shootarrow(dir);
  }
}

// Serverside version using shoot() function (not level.shoot())
function shootMultiArrowServer(count, spread) {
  temp.baseDir = player.dir * pi / 2;
  temp.angleStep = spread / (count - 1);
  temp.startAngle = baseDir - spread / 2;
  
  for (temp.i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    temp.angle = startAngle + i * angleStep;
    // shoot() is a global function, takes: x, y, z, angle, zangle, power, gani, aniparams
    shoot(player.x, player.y, 0, angle, 0, 5, "arrow.gani", "MultiShot");
    // Note: "MultiShot" is a shootparam that could trigger onActionMultiShot() handlers
  }
}
```

```gs2
  // First in chain

  // Second in chain

  // Final in chain
```

```gs2
  if (player.guild == "Warriors") {
    this.handleWarriorInteraction();
  } else if (player.guild == "Mages") {
```

#### Complete GUI Control Reference

**Common GUI Control Properties:**
All GUI controls inherit basic properties from `GuiControl`:
- `x`, `y` - Position (integer pixels)
- `width`, `height` - Size (integer pixels)
- `visible` - Visibility (boolean)
- `active` - Whether control is active (boolean)
- `profile` - Control profile object or string
- `altcommand` - Alternative command string
- `tooltip` - Tooltip text string
- `tooltipProfile` - Tooltip profile object

**Layout Properties:**
- `horizSizing` - Horizontal sizing mode ("right", "width", "relative")
- `vertSizing` - Vertical sizing mode ("bottom", "height", "relative")
- `minExtent` - Minimum size string "{width, height}"
- `maxExtent` - Maximum size string "{width, height}"
- `canMove` - Can be moved by user (boolean)
- `canClose` - Can be closed by user (boolean)
- `canResize` - Can be resized by user (boolean)
- `canMinimize` - Can be minimized (boolean)
- `canMaximize` - Can be maximized (boolean)

**GuiWindowCtrl Properties:**
- `text` - Window title text
- `closeCommand` - Command when window closes
- `resizeWidth` - Allow width resizing (boolean)
- `resizeHeight` - Allow height resizing (boolean)
- `minimized` - Is minimized (boolean)
- `maximized` - Is maximized (boolean)
- `setTitle(str)` - Set window title

**GuiButtonCtrl Properties:**
- `text` - Button text
- `buttonType` - Button type ("PushButton", "ToggleButton", "RadioButton")
- `groupNum` - Radio button group number (integer)
- `depressed` - Is depressed/toggled (boolean)
- `autoSize` - Auto-size to text (boolean)

**GuiTextCtrl Properties:**
- `text` - Text content
- `maxLength` - Maximum text length (integer)
- `password` - Display as password (boolean)
- `deniedSound` - Sound when input denied
- `textProfile` - Text profile object

**GuiTextEditCtrl Properties (extends GuiTextCtrl):**
- `cursorPosition` - Cursor position (integer)
- `highlightColor` - Text highlight color string
- `validate` - Validation callback function name

**GuiScrollCtrl Properties:**
- `willFirstRespond` - Gets focus (boolean)
- `hScrollBar` - Horizontal scrollbar control (object)
- `vScrollBar` - Vertical scrollbar control (object)
- `scrollProfile` - Scroll profile object
- `contentControl` - Content control object

**GuiListCtrl Properties:**
- `columns` - Column headers array
- `rowSize` - Row height (integer)
- `selectedRow` - Currently selected row (integer)
- `selectedRows` - Array of selected row indices
- `hilightRow` - Highlighted row (integer)
- `allowMultipleSelection` - Allow multiple row selection (boolean)

**GuiListCtrl Methods:**
- `addRow(id, text, iconIndex, iconName)` - Add row, returns row number
- `clear()` - Clear all rows
- `removeRow(id)` - Remove row by ID
- `getRowText(id)` - Get row text by ID
- `setRowText(id, text)` - Set row text
- `getRowNumById(id)` - Get row number by ID
- `getSelectedId()` - Get selected row ID
- `setSelectedRow(row)` - Set selected row

**GuiCheckBoxCtrl Properties:**
- `text` - Checkbox text
- `checked` - Is checked (boolean)
- `stateOn` - Value when checked (integer, default 1)
- `stateOff` - Value when unchecked (integer, default 0)

**GuiSliderCtrl Properties:**
- `value` - Current value (float)
- `minValue` - Minimum value (float)
- `maxValue` - Maximum value (float)
- `tickMark` - Tick mark spacing (float)
- `ticksPerSide` - Ticks per side (integer)

**GuiBitmapCtrl Properties:**
- `bitmap` - Image filename (string)
- `wrap` - Wrap image (boolean)
- `flipX` - Flip horizontally (boolean)
- `flipY` - Flip vertically (boolean)
- `color` - Image color tint string

**GuiChunkedBitmapCtrl Properties (extends GuiBitmapCtrl):**
- `bitmapArray` - Array of image filenames for chunks
- `chunksHoriz` - Horizontal chunk count (integer)
- `chunksVert` - Vertical chunk count (integer)

**GuiProgressCtrl Properties:**
- `progress` - Progress value (0.0 to 1.0)
- `text` - Progress text
- `mode` - Progress mode ("horiz", "vert", "circular")

**GuiColorPickerCtrl Properties:**
- `baseColor` - Base color string "r g b a"
- `selectorColor` - Selector color string
- `pickColor` - Picked color string

**GuiCanvas Properties (Clientside):**
- `useWindowMouseEvents` - Capture window mouse events (boolean)
- `renderMode` - Render mode string

**Basic GUI Example:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  // Create window using new GuiWindowCtrl
  this.mainWindow = new GuiWindowCtrl("MainWindow") {
    profile = "GuiWindowProfile";
    x = 50;
    y = 50;
    width = 300;
    height = 200;
    text = "My Window";
  };
  
  // Create button
  this.button = new GuiButtonCtrl("MyButton") {
    profile = "GuiButtonProfile";
    x = 10;
    y = 30;
    width = 100;
    height = 25;
    text = "Click Me";
  };
}
```

#### Advanced String Formatting and Templates

**String Formatting Functions:**
```gs2
// format2() - Format string (if available on server)
temp.text = "Player " @ player.name @ " has " @ player.rupees @ " rupees and " @ player.hearts @ " hearts";

// String interpolation (GS2 syntax)
temp.name = "John";
temp.rupees = 500;
temp.text = "Player " @ name @ " has " @ rupees @ " rupees";
```

**Template String Patterns:**
```gs2
// Simple template
function formatMessage(template, data) {
  temp.result = template;
  // Manually track data keys (getstringkeys only works on variable prefixes)
  // Assume data keys are known, or pass keys separately
  temp.dataKeys = {"player", "coins"}; // Define manually or pass as parameter
  
  for (temp.i = 0; i < dataKeys.size(); i++) {
    temp.key = dataKeys[i];
    if (data[key] != null) {
      temp.value = data[key];
      result = result.replace("{{" @ key @ "}}", value);
    }
  }
  return result;
}

temp.message = "Welcome " @ player.name @ "! You have " @ player.rupees @ " coins.";

// Advanced template with conditionals
function formatTemplate(template, data) {
  temp.result = template;
  
  // Replace variables (manually track keys - getstringkeys only works on variable prefixes)
  temp.dataKeys = {"player", "coins", "level"}; // Define manually or pass as parameter
  for (temp.i = 0; i < dataKeys.size(); i++) {
    temp.key = dataKeys[i];
    if (data[key] != null) {
      result = result.replace("{{" @ key @ "}}", data[key]);
    }
  }
  
  // Replace conditionals {{if key}}...{{endif}}
  while (result.pos("{{if ") >= 0) {
    temp.ifStart = result.pos("{{if ");
    temp.ifEnd = result.find("}}", ifStart);
    temp.key = result.substring(ifStart + 5, ifEnd);
    temp.ifContentStart = ifEnd + 2;
    temp.ifContentEnd = result.find("{{endif}}", ifContentStart);
    
    if (data[key] != null && data[key] != "") {
      temp.content = result.substring(ifContentStart, ifContentEnd);
      result = result.substring(0, ifStart) @ content @ result.substring(ifContentEnd + 9);
    } else {
      result = result.substring(0, ifStart) @ result.substring(ifContentEnd + 9);
    }
  }
  
  return result;
}
```

**String Padding and Alignment:**
```gs2
// Pad string to length
function padLeft(str, length, char) {
  temp.result = str;
  while (result.length() < length) {
    result = char @ result;
  }
  return result;
}

function padRight(str, length, char) {
  temp.result = str;
  while (result.length() < length) {
    result = result @ char;
  }
  return result;
}

// Center string
function padCenter(str, length, char) {
  temp.padding = length - str.length();
  temp.leftPad = padding / 2;
  temp.rightPad = padding - leftPad;
  return padLeft("", leftPad, char) @ str @ padRight("", rightPad, char);
}

// Examples
temp.text1 = padLeft("42", 5, "0");  // "00042"
temp.text2 = padRight("Hello", 10, " ");  // "Hello     "
temp.text3 = padCenter("Title", 15, " ");  // "     Title     "
```

**String Truncation:**
```gs2
// Truncate with ellipsis
function truncate(str, maxLength) {
  if (str.length() <= maxLength) {
    return str;
  }
  return str.substring(0, maxLength - 3) @ "...";
}

// Truncate at word boundary
function truncateWords(str, maxLength) {
  if (str.length() <= maxLength) {
    return str;
  }
  temp.truncated = str.substring(0, maxLength);
  temp.lastSpace = truncated.lastindexof(" ");
  if (lastSpace > 0) {
    return truncated.substring(0, lastSpace) @ "...";
  }
  return truncated @ "...";
}
```

#### Advanced Error Handling and Debugging

**Try-Catch Pattern (Simulated):**
```gs2
// GS2 doesn't have try-catch, but you can use flags and checks
function safeDivide(a, b) {
  if (b == 0) {
    echo("Error: Division by zero");
    return 0;
  }
  return a / b;
}

// Safe array access
function safeArrayAccess(array, index) {
  if (array == null) {
    echo("Error: Array is null");
    return null;
  }
  if (index < 0 || index >= array.size()) {
    echo("Error: Index out of bounds");
    return null;
  }
  return array[index];
}
```


**Debug Logging System:**
```gs2
function onCreated() {
  this.debugEnabled = true;
  this.debugLevel = 1; // 0=none, 1=errors, 2=warnings, 3=info, 4=verbose
}

function debug(level, category, message) {
  if (!debugEnabled || level > debugLevel) return;
  
  temp.prefix = "";
  if (level == 1) prefix = "[ERROR]";
  else if (level == 2) prefix = "[WARN]";
  else if (level == 3) prefix = "[INFO]";
  else if (level == 4) prefix = "[VERBOSE]";
  
  temp.timestamp = formattimestring("%H:%M:%S", timevar2);
  temp.logMessage = "[" @ timestamp @ "] " @ prefix @ " [" @ category @ "] " @ message;
  
  echo(logMessage);
  
  // Optional: Write to file
  temp.logEntry = logMessage @ "\n";
  logEntry.savestring("logs/debug.log", 1); // Append
}

// Usage
debug(3, "NPC", "NPC created at " @ this.x @ ", " @ this.y);
debug(1, "ERROR", "Failed to load player data");
debug(4, "PATHFINDING", "Calculating path from " @ startX @ "," @ startY @ " to " @ endX @ "," @ endY);
```

#### Security Best Practices

**SQL Injection Prevention:**
```gs2
// Always use parameterized queries
// BAD:
temp.query = "SELECT * FROM players WHERE account = '" @ account @ "'";
// This is vulnerable to SQL injection

// GOOD:
temp.result = requestsql("SELECT * FROM players WHERE account = ?", account);
// Parameterized queries prevent SQL injection
```

**Access Control:**
```gs2
function hasPermission(player, permission) {
  // Using player.hasright(path, directory) - checks if player has read/write access to directory
  
  if (permission == "edit_levels") {
    return player.hasright("rw", "levels/");
  }
  
  if (permission == "manage_players") {
    return player.hasright("rw", "scripts/");
  }
  
  if (permission == "moderate_chat") {
    return player.hasright("rw", "chat/");
  }
  
  // Custom fake folder rights (commonly used pattern: _/folder_name)
  // These are virtual folders that don't exist on disk but are used for permission checks
  if (permission == "npc_channel_chat") {
    return player.hasright("rw", "_/npcchannelchat");
  }
  
  if (permission == "server_management") {
    return player.hasright("rw", "_/servers_all");
  }
  
  if (permission == "specific_server") {
    // Dynamic server name check
    return player.hasright("rw", "_/servers_" @ this.serverName);
  }
  
  return false;
}

// Example usage with hasright() directly
function checkPlayerRights() {
  // Check if player has rights to scripts directory
  if (player.hasright("rw", "scripts/")) {
    chat = "You have script access!";
  } else {
    chat = "Access denied.";
  }
}

// Example: Using custom fake folder rights (pattern: _/folder_name)
function checkNPCChatAccess(channelName) {
  // Check custom right for NPC channel chat (e.g., rw _/npcchannelchat)
  if (player.hasright("rw", "_/npc" @ channelName)) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  if (!checkNPCChatAccess("channelchat")) {
    chat = "You need rw _/npcchannelchat to use this!";
    return;
  }
  chat = "NPC channel access granted!";
}

// Usage with custom permission system
function onPlayerTouchsMe() {
  if (!hasPermission(player, "edit_levels")) {
    chat = "You don't have permission to do that";
    return;
  }
  
  // Perform restricted action
}
```

#### Advanced triggerclient() Patterns

**Using player.triggerClient() for Targeted Communication:**
```gs2
// Server-side: Trigger specific player's weapon/GUI script
function healPlayer(account) {
  temp.pl = findplayer(account);
  if (pl != null) {
    // Trigger client-side weapon script "-HPSystem" to remove death GUI
    // Syntax: player.triggerClient("gui" or "weapon", scriptName, functionName, ...params)
    pl.triggerClient("weapon", "-HPSystem", "removeGUI");
  }
}

// Alternative: Using triggerclient() with account string
function notifyPlayer(account) {
  triggerclient("weapon", account, "-HPSystem", "removeGUI");
}

// Client-side weapon script "-HPSystem"
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onActionClientside(cmd) {
  if (cmd == "removeGUI") {
    // Check if death UI exists before destroying
    if (isObject(deathUI)) {
      deathUI.destroy();
    }
  }
}

// Alternative: Using switch statement
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onActionClientside(cmd) {
  switch (cmd) {
    case "removeGUI":
      if (isObject("deathUI")) {
        deathUI.destroy();
      }
      break;
  }
}
```

#### Spawn and Respawn Systems

**Simple Respawn System:**
```gs2
function onCreated() {
  this.spawnX = this.x;
  this.spawnY = this.y;
  this.spawnDelay = 5.0;
  this.isDead = false;
}

function onWasHit(damage) {
  this.hearts -= damage;
  if (this.hearts <= 0 && !isDead) {
    this.die();
  }
}

function die() {
  this.isDead = true;
  this.hide();
  this.hearts = 0;
  
  // Schedule respawn
  scheduleevent(spawnDelay, "respawn");
}

function respawn() {
  this.isDead = false;
  this.x = spawnX;
  this.y = spawnY;
  this.hearts = this.maxhearts;
  this.show();
  chat = "I have returned!";
}
```

```gs2
function saveToDatabase(account, data) {

  temp.jsonData = ""; // Build JSON string manually or use available serialization
  temp.timestamp = int(timevar2);
  
  requestsql("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO player_data (account, data, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", 
             account, jsonData, timestamp);
}

function loadFromDatabase(account) {
  temp.result = requestsql("SELECT data FROM player_data WHERE account = ?", account);
  if (result.size() > 0) {
    temp.jsonData = result[0].data;
    // Note: JSON parsing may need to be done manually or via server-specific functions
    return jsonData;
  }
  return null;
}
```

#### Random Generation Systems



**Random Name Generation:**
```gs2
function generateName() {
  temp.prefixes = {"Red", "Blue", "Green", "Dark", "Light", "Swift", "Strong"};
  temp.names = {"Warrior", "Knight", "Mage", "Rogue", "Hunter", "Priest"};
  temp.suffixes = {"of Fire", "of Ice", "the Brave", "the Wise", "the Strong"};
  
  // random(0, 1) returns float - use < 0.5 for 50/50 boolean chance
  temp.usePrefix = random(0, 1) < 0.5;
  temp.useSuffix = random(0, 1) < 0.5;
  
  temp.result = ""; // Note: 'name' is reserved - refers to object/function name
  if (usePrefix) {
    result = prefixes[int(random(0, prefixes.size() - 1))] @ " ";
  }
  
  result @= names[int(random(0, names.size() - 1))];
  
  if (useSuffix) {
    result @= " " @ suffixes[int(random(0, suffixes.size() - 1))];
  }
  
  return result;
}
```

---

#### Optimization and Performance


**Spatial Partitioning:**
```gs2
function onCreated() {
  this.spatialGrid = {};
  this.gridSize = 10; // 10x10 tile cells
}

function getGridCell(x, y) {
  temp.cellX = floor(x / gridSize);
  temp.cellY = floor(y / gridSize);
  return cellX @ "," @ cellY;
}

function addToGrid(obj, x, y) {
  temp.cell = getGridCell(x, y);
  if (spatialGrid[cell] == null) {
    spatialGrid[cell] = {};
  }
  spatialGrid[cell][obj.id] = obj;
}

function getObjectsInArea(x, y, radius) {
  temp.minCellX = floor((x - radius) / gridSize);
  temp.maxCellX = floor((x + radius) / gridSize);
  temp.minCellY = floor((y - radius) / gridSize);
  temp.maxCellY = floor((y + radius) / gridSize);
  
  temp.results = {};
  for (temp.cellX = minCellX; cellX <= maxCellX; cellX++) {
    for (temp.cellY = minCellY; cellY <= maxCellY; cellY++) {
      temp.cell = cellX @ "," @ cellY;
      if (spatialGrid[cell] != null) {
        temp.cellObjects = spatialGrid[cell];
        // Iterate using numeric indices - assumes objects are stored with numeric keys
        // If using string keys, track them separately when adding objects
        for (temp.i = 0; i < cellObjects.size(); i++) {
          if (cellObjects[i] != null) {
            temp.obj = cellObjects[i];
            if (obj != null && obj.id != null) {
              results[obj.id] = obj;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  return results;
}
```


---

#### Complete Time and Date Functions

**Time Variables:**
- `timevar` - integer (read only) - Current server time in seconds (continuous, can be large numbers)
- `timevar2` - float (read only) - Current time as Unix timestamp (seconds since 1970-01-01)

**Time Functions:**
- `getutctime()` - returns integer - Get current UTC time in seconds since 1970-01-01 (same as `int(timevar2)` on server-side)
- `int(timevar2)` - Get Unix timestamp as integer
- `formattimestring(format, time)` - returns string - Format time string using format specifiers (like Unix strftime)
- `getfilemodtime(str)` - returns integer - Get file modification time (Unix time compatible with timevar2)

**Time Format Specifiers (for formattimestring):**
```gs2
// Common format patterns
temp.now = timevar2;
temp.formatted = formattimestring("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", now);
// Output: "2024-01-15 14:30:45"

// Available specifiers:
// %Y - 4-digit year (2024)
// %y - 2-digit year (24)
// %m - Month (01-12)
// %d - Day of month (01-31)
// %H - Hour 24-hour (00-23)
// %I - Hour 12-hour (01-12)
// %M - Minute (00-59)
// %S - Second (00-59)
// %p - AM/PM
// %A - Full weekday name (Monday)
// %a - Abbreviated weekday (Mon)
// %B - Full month name (January)
// %b - Abbreviated month (Jan)
// %w - Weekday as number (0=Sunday, 6=Saturday)
```

**Time Examples:**
```gs2
// Get current time
temp.currentTime = timevar2;
temp.formatted = formattimestring("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", currentTime);

// Get Unix timestamp as integer
temp.unixTime = getutctime(); // or int(timevar2)

// Calculate elapsed time
temp.startTime = timevar2;
// ... do work ...
temp.elapsed = timevar2 - startTime;
echo("Operation took " @ elapsed @ " seconds");


// Time differences
function getTimeDifference(time1, time2) {
  temp.diff = abs(time2 - time1);
  temp.days = floor(diff / 86400);
  temp.hours = floor((diff % 86400) / 3600);
  temp.minutes = floor((diff % 3600) / 60);
  temp.seconds = diff % 60;
  
  // GS2 arrays: use string keys
  // GS2 arrays don't support string key assignments - return values directly or use separate variables
  // Option: Return as separate values or use numeric indices
  // Create pre-sized array, then assign to indices
  temp.result = new[5];
  result[0] = diff;      // total
  result[1] = days;
  result[2] = hours;
  result[3] = minutes;
  result[4] = seconds;
  
  // Alternative: Use .add() method with empty array
  // temp.result = {};
  // result.add(diff);
  // result.add(days);
  // result.add(hours);
  // result.add(minutes);
  // result.add(seconds);
  
  return result;
}
```

#### Weapon Management Functions

**Weapon Functions:**
- `addweapon(str)` - Add weapon to player by script name
- `TServerPlayer.addweapon(str)` - Add weapon to specific player
- `removeweapon(str)` - Remove weapon from player by script name
- `TServerPlayer.removeweapon(str)` - Remove weapon from specific player

**Note:** `setweapon`, `getweapon`, and `hasweapon` functions do not exist in standard GS2. Weapon management is typically handled through `addweapon` and `removeweapon` only.

#### Advanced Communication Patterns

**Complete Trigger System:**
```gs2
// triggeraction(x, y, action, params) - Tile-based action trigger
// triggerserver(rcname, target, function, ...args) - Client to server (rcname: "gui", "weapon", "npc")
// triggerclient(rcname, target, function, ...args) - Server to client (rcname: "gui", "weapon")

// Server-side NPC
function onCreated() {
  this.name = "RemoteNPC";
}

function onActionLeftMouse() {
  // Trigger action at player position
  triggeraction(player.x, player.y, "ChestOpen", "");
}

// Client-side weapon receives triggeraction
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onActionChestOpen() {
  echo("Chest opened at " @ player.x @ ", " @ player.y);
}

// Level-wide communication (alternative - use triggeraction)
function broadcastMessage(message) {
  // Broadcast to all players in area using triggeraction
  for (temp.pl : findareaplayers(this.x - 50, this.y - 50, 100, 100)) {
    triggeraction(pl.x, pl.y, "ReceiveMessage", this.name @ "," @ message);
  }
}

// Handler for triggeraction
function onActionReceiveMessage(params) {
  temp.parts = params.split(",");
  temp.sender = parts[0];
  temp.message = parts.substring(sender.length() + 1);
  chat = sender @ " says: " @ message;
}

// Server-to-client examples

// Example 1: Server NPC to client GUI
function notifyPlayer(message) {
  triggerclient("gui", player.account, "showMessage", message);
}

// Client-side GUI script
//#CLIENTSIDE
function showMessage(message) {
  echo("Server says: " @ message);
}

// Example 2: Server NPC to client weapon
function notifyWeapon(message) {
  triggerclient("weapon", player.account, "showNotification", message);
}

// Client-side weapon script
//#CLIENTSIDE
function showNotification(message) {
  echo("Notification: " @ message);
}

// Client-to-server examples

// Example 1: Client weapon to server NPC database
//#CLIENTSIDE
function requestItem(itemName) {
  triggerserver("npc", "ShopDB", "giveItem", player.account, itemName);
}

// Server-side NPC database (name must be "ShopDB")
function giveItem(account, itemName) {
  temp.pl = findplayer(account);
  if (pl != null) {
    chat = "Here's your " @ itemName;
  }
}

// Example 2: Client weapon to server weapon
//#CLIENTSIDE
function requestServerData() {
  triggerserver("weapon", "ServerWeapon", "getPlayerData", player.account);
}

// Server-side weapon script (name must be "ServerWeapon")
function getPlayerData(account) {
  temp.pl = findplayer(account);
  if (pl != null) {
    triggerclient("weapon", account, "playerData", pl.hearts, pl.rupees);
  }
}

// Example 3: Client GUI to server NPC
//#CLIENTSIDE
function requestItem(itemName) {
  triggerserver("gui", "NPCName", "giveItem", player.account, itemName);
}

// Server-side NPC script (name must be "NPCName")
function giveItem(account, itemName) {
  temp.pl = findplayer(account);
  if (pl != null) {
    chat = "Here's your " @ itemName;
  }
}
```

```gs2
  // Double click on NPC
```

**Combat Events:**
```gs2
function onWasHit(damage) {
  // NPC was hit (damage is the damage amount)
  this.hearts -= damage;
}

function onWasAttacked(attacker) {
  // NPC was attacked (attacker is the player/NPC that attacked)
  // Different from onWasHit - this fires even if no damage was done
}

function onActionProjectile2(projectile) {
  // Projectile hit this NPC
  // projectile object contains: x, y, power, owner, etc.
}
```

```gs2
// Note: Event handlers for movement may vary by server implementation

  // Called when move() completes (if event flag 8 was set)
}

function onBlocked() {
  // Called when movement is blocked (may be server-specific)
}
```

```gs2
  // Called when timer expires (set with settimer())

  // Called when scheduled event fires (set with scheduleevent())
```

```gs2
  // Called when triggeraction(x, y, "ActionName", params) is called
  // Example: onActionChestOpen() for triggeraction(..., "ChestOpen", ...)
  // Note: onCreated() is a standard event handler and has nothing to do with trigger actions
```

#### Complete Clientside Image System Deep Dive

**Image Creation and Management:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  // Basic image display
  temp.img = showimg(1, "sprite.png", 10, 20);
  
  // Image with Z coordinate
  temp.img2 = showimg2(2, "sprite.png", 15, 25, 0.5);
  
  // Find existing image
  temp.foundImg = findimg(1);
  if (foundImg != null) {
    foundImg.x = 20;
    foundImg.y = 30;
  }
  
  // Hide images
  hideimg(1); // Hide single image
  hideimgs(1, 10); // Hide range of images (indices 1-10)
}
```

**Complete Image Properties:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
temp.img = showimg(1, "sprite.png", 0, 0);

// Position and transformation
img.x = 10.5;              // X position (float)
img.y = 20.3;              // Y position (float)
img.z = 0.0;               // Z depth (float)
img.zoom = 1.5;            // Zoom factor (float)
img.rotation = pi/4;       // Rotation in radians
img.stretchx = 1.2;       // Horizontal stretch
img.stretchy = 0.8;        // Vertical stretch
img.spin = 0.1;            // Rotation speed per frame

// Color and transparency
img.red = 1.0;             // Red component (0.0-1.0)
img.green = 0.8;           // Green component
img.blue = 0.6;            // Blue component
img.alpha = 0.9;           // Transparency (0.0-1.0)

// Drawing settings
img.layer = 10;            // Drawing layer (higher = on top)
img.mode = 1;              // Drawing mode (0=add, 1=trans, 2=subtract, 3=day/night)
img.dimension = 0;         // Dimension/layer group

// Sprite sheet support
img.partx = 0;             // X offset in sprite sheet
img.party = 0;             // Y offset in sprite sheet
img.partw = 32;            // Width of sprite to show
img.parth = 32;            // Height of sprite to show

// Image source
img.image = "sprite.png";  // Image filename
img.width = 32.0;          // Image width (float)
img.height = 32.0;         // Image height (float)

// Animation
img.ani = "walk.gani";     // Animation file
img.dir = 0;               // Direction for animation (0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right)

// Text rendering (if image is used for text)
img.font = "Arial";        // Font name
img.style = "Bold";        // Font style
img.text = "Hello";        // Text content
img.code = "Arial@Bold@Hello"; // Old font format

// Attachment
img.attachtoowner = false; // Attach to owner object
img.attachoffset = "{x, y, z}"; // Attachment offset string
img.playerlook = false;    // Use owner's appearance

// Particles
img.emitter = null;        // Particle emitter object (read-only)
img.sound = "";            // Particle sound file
img.uniqueparticle = false; // Each particle stores own data

// Advanced
img.useowncenter = false;  // Use custom rotation center
img.rotationcenter = "{x, y}"; // Custom rotation center
img.shadowcolor = "";      // Shadow color
img.shadowoffset = "{x, y}"; // Shadow offset
img.textshadow = false;    // Enable text shadow
img.flashfile = "";        // Flash animation file
```

**Image Animation System:**
```gs2
//#CLIENTSIDE
function onCreated() {
  this.frame = 0;
  this.maxFrames = 8;
  this.frameTime = 0.1;
  
  temp.img = showimg(1, "spritesheet.png", player.x, player.y);
  img.layer = 1;
  img.partw = 32;
  img.parth = 32;
  
  scheduleevent(frameTime, "animateSprite");
}

function animateSprite() {
  temp.img = findimg(1);
  if (img != null) {
    // Calculate sprite sheet position
    temp.cols = 4; // 4 columns in sprite sheet
    temp.col = frame % cols;
    temp.row = floor(frame / cols);
    
    img.partx = col * 32;
    img.party = row * 32;
    
    frame++;
    if (frame >= maxFrames) {
      frame = 0;
    }
    
    scheduleevent(frameTime, "animateSprite");
  }
}
```

**Complete Trigger System Documentation:**
```gs2
// triggerclient(rcname, target, function, ...args)
// Server-side: Send data to client

// Trigger GUI script
triggerclient("gui", player.account, "updateUI", player.hearts, player.rupees);

// Trigger weapon script
triggerclient("weapon", player.account, "updateWeaponStats", 10, 5);

// triggerserver(rcname, target, function, ...args)  
// Client-side: Send data to server

// GUI script to server NPC
//#CLIENTSIDE
triggerserver("gui", this.name, "requestData", player.account);

// Weapon script to server NPC database
//#CLIENTSIDE
triggerserver("npc", "ShopDB", "buyItem", player.account, "sword");

// Weapon script to server weapon
//#CLIENTSIDE
triggerserver("weapon", "ServerWeapon", "getData", player.account);

// triggeraction(x, y, action, params)
// Position-based action trigger
triggeraction(player.x, player.y, "ChestOpen", "");
```

## Cryptography and Hashing Functions (TEncryption)

All functions in this section are **global** — available on both client and server. Source class: `TEncryption` (TEncryption_WolfSSL.cpp).

### MD5 Hash
`md5(string)` — returns string
- Computes MD5 hash of input string.
```gs2
temp.hash = md5("hello");
// Returns: "5d41402abc4b2a76b9719d911017c592"
```

### SHA-256 Hash
`sha256(string)` — returns string
- Computes SHA-256 hash of input string.
```gs2
temp.hash = sha256("hello");
// Returns hex digest string
```

### Checksum
`checksum(string)` — returns float
- Calculates a numeric checksum of a string. Used for comparing data integrity.
```gs2
temp.check = checksum(this.grouplists[i]);
```

### Base64 Encode/Decode
`base64encode(string)` — returns string
`base64decode(string)` — returns string
- Base64 encoding/decoding for binary-safe data transmission.
```gs2
temp.encoded = base64encode(player.account);
temp.decoded = base64decode(temp.encoded);

// Nested hashing for obfuscation
code = md5(md5(md5(base64encode(code))));
```

### AES Encryption (Advanced)
`aes_encrypt(key, iv, plaintext)` — returns string
`aes_decrypt(key, iv, ciphertext)` — returns string
- AES symmetric encryption/decryption. Requires key and IV strings.
```gs2
temp.ciphertext = aes_encrypt(this.key, iv, plaintext);
temp.plaintext = aes_decrypt(this.key, iv, ciphertext);
```

### DES Encryption (Advanced)
`des_encrypt(key, plaintext)` — returns string
`des_decrypt(key, ciphertext)` — returns string
- DES symmetric encryption/decryption.

### RC4 Cipher
`rc4_cipher(key, data)` — returns string
- RC4 stream cipher. Same function encrypts and decrypts (symmetric).
```gs2
data = rc4_cipher(key, data);  // Encrypt
data = rc4_cipher(key, data);  // Decrypt (same call)

// Real usage from Unholy Nation encryption system
data = rc4_cipher(oldKey, data);
```

### RSA Functions (Advanced)
`rsa_encrypt(publickey, plaintext)` — returns string
`rsa_decrypt(privatekey, ciphertext)` — returns string
`rsa_sign(privatekey, data)` — returns string
`rsa_verify(publickey, data, signature)` — returns bool
`rsa_encodesignature(key, data)` — returns string
- RSA public-key cryptography for signatures and encryption.
```gs2
// Signature verification
if (rsa_verify(getPublicKey(), md5("signedData"), temp.signature)) {
  echo("Signature is valid!");
}

// Modern SHA-256 signature
if (!rsa_verify(base64decode(PUB), sha256(timestamp), signature)) {
  return;  // Bad signature
}
```

---

## HTTP and Web Request Functions (THTTPRequest)

All functions in this section are **global**. Source class: `THTTPRequest` (THTTPRequest.cpp).

**⚠️ IMPORTANT: HTTP request functions are LOGIN SERVER RESTRICTED. They were disabled around 2008 for abuse prevention and only work on login servers with restricted access.**

### requesturl
`requesturl(url)` — returns object
- Makes an asynchronous HTTP GET request. Returns a request object.
- Use `waitfor(req, "onReceiveData", timeout)` to wait for completion.
- Access `req.data` for response content, `req.returncode` for HTTP status.
```gs2
temp.req = requesturl("https://api.ipify.org/");
waitfor(temp.req, "onReceiveData", 30);
echo(temp.req.data);
```

### requesthttp
`requesthttp(url, method, data)` — returns object
- Makes an HTTP request with specified method (0=GET, 1=POST) and optional POST data.
```gs2
temp.req = requesthttp("http://example.com/api", 1, "key=value");
```

### gethttprequest
`gethttprequest(url, method, data)` — returns object
- Alternative HTTP request function.

### gethttprequests
`gethttprequests()` — returns object
- Returns array of all active HTTP request objects.

### requesturlasgamefile
`requesturlasgamefile(url, filename, flags, useTempFile)` — returns object
- Downloads a URL and saves as a game file for client distribution.

### getlastwebrequesttime / getlastwebdownloadtime
`getlastwebrequesttime()` — returns float (time in seconds since last web request)
`getlastwebdownloadtime()` — returns float (time since last download completed)

### urlencode / urldecode
`urlencode(string)` — returns string
`urldecode(string)` — returns string
- Percent-encodes/decodes strings for safe URL parameter transmission.
```gs2
temp.url = "https://glosbe.com/gapi/translate?from=eng&dest=pol&format=json&phrase=" @ urlencode(temp.message);

// Decode URL-encoded query parameters
temp.request.query.(@ temp.param[0]) = urldecode(temp.param[1]);
```

## Network and Socket Functions (TSocket / TSocketConnection)

These functions require TSocket objects (created via `new TSocket("Name")`).

### TSocket Object Methods

#### bind
`socket.bind(port, isUdp)` — returns void
- Binds socket to specified port. Set `isUdp` to true for UDP.

#### connect
`socket.connect(host, port)` — returns void
- Connects socket to host:port (TCP).

#### sendudp
`socket.sendudp(data, ip, port)` — returns void
- Sends UDP data to specified IP and port.

### DNS Functions (TSocketConnection)

#### resolvehost
`resolvehost(hostname)` — returns string
- DNS lookup — resolves hostname to IP address.
```gs2
temp.ip = resolvehost("example.com");
```

#### adddnsentry
`adddnsentry(hostname, ip)` — returns void
- Adds a custom DNS entry mapping hostname to IP address.

### TSocket Events
```gs2
function onConnect() { }        // Connection established
function onConnectFailed() { }  // Connection failed
function onClose() { }          // Connection closed
function onNewClient(socket) { } // New client connected (server socket)
function onReceiveData(data) { } // Received TCP data
function onReceiveDataPackage(package) { } // Received delimited data package
function onReceiveUDPData(data, senderIP, senderPort) { } // Received UDP data
function onBind() { }           // Socket bind successful
function onBindFailed() { }     // Socket bind failed
```

## Time System Functions (TTime) — Complete Reference

Source class: `TTime` (TTime.cpp).

### getutctime
`getutctime()` — returns int
- Returns current UTC time in seconds since 1970-01-01 (Unix timestamp).
- Equivalent to `int(timevar2)` on the server-side.
```gs2
echo("Current time: " @ formattimestring("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", getutctime()));
```

### formattimestring
`formattimestring(format, time)` — returns string
- Formats a Unix timestamp using strftime-style format specifiers.
- See the **Time Format Specifiers** section earlier in this document for all specifiers.
```gs2
temp.formatted = formattimestring("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", timevar2);
// Output: "2024-01-15 14:30:45"
```

### converttimetostring
`converttimetostring(time)` — returns string
- Converts Unix timestamp to a human-readable date string.
```gs2
temp.dateStr = converttimetostring(timevar2).tokenize();
// Output: "Mon Jan 15 14:30:45 2024" (tokenized into words)
```

### convertstringtotime
`convertstringtotime(format, datestring)` — returns float
- Parses a date string using the given format and returns Unix timestamp.
- Inverse of `formattimestring()`.
```gs2
// Parse HTTP Last-Modified header
temp.timestamp = convertstringtotime("%a, %d %b %Y %H:%M:%S %z", temp.header);

// Parse database timestamp
temp.unixtime = convertstringtotime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", temp.time);

// Parse file modification time
temp.f.iwrite = convertstringtotime(f.fwrite, "%-m/%e/%Y %-I:%M %p");
```

### getFrameTick / adventure_getframetick
`getFrameTick()` — returns int (HIDDEN)
- Gets current frame tick rate in milliseconds.

### adventure_setframetick
`adventure_setframetick(ms)` — returns void (Privileged)
- Sets frame tick rate.

---

- Sets sound pitch based on musical note and octave.

- `updateMp3Volume()` — Update MP3 volume
