## Screenshot Functions

### makescreenshot2 [HIDDEN]
`makescreenshot2(filename, flags, x, y, z, width, height)` — returns void
- Takes a screenshot of a rectangular area of the game world.
- `flags`: 0x4 = game world rendering mode
```gs2
makescreenshot2("temp_mapgenerate.jpg", 0x4, x, y, 0, 512, 512);
makescreenshot2("capture.jpg", 0x4, x-32, y-32, 0, 1024, 1024);
```

### makescreenshotgame
`makescreenshotgame(filename, flags, width, height)` — returns void
- Takes a screenshot of the game screen at specified resolution.
```gs2
makescreenshotgame("capture.jpg", 0x3f, 1400, 1080);
```

### makescreenshotfull
`makescreenshotfull(filename, flags, width_or_neg1)` — returns void
- Takes a full screenshot. Use -1 for auto-detect dimensions.
```gs2
makescreenshotfull("era_test-camera.png", -1, -1);
```

---

## Image Pixel Inspection Functions

### getimgpixel
`getimgpixel(filename, x, y)` — returns object
- Gets pixel color at coordinates in an image file. Returns `{red, green, blue, alpha}`.
```gs2
temp.color = getimgpixel(player.body, 77, 22);
temp.img_color = getimgpixel("state.png", player.ap-1, 120);
```

### isimgpixeltransparent
`isimgpixeltransparent(filename, x, y)` — returns bool
- Checks if a pixel in an image file is transparent.
```gs2
if (isimgpixeltransparent("sprite.png", j, i)) {
  // Pixel is transparent
}
```

### isimgrectangletransparent
`isimgrectangletransparent(filename, x, y, width, height)` — returns bool
- Checks if an entire rectangle in an image is transparent.

### getimgwidth / getimgheight
`getimgwidth(filename)` — returns int
`getimgheight(filename)` — returns int
- Gets pixel dimensions of an image file.
```gs2
this.setshape(1, getimgwidth(this.image), getimgheight(this.image));
```

---

## Text Measurement Functions

### gettextwidth
`gettextwidth(zoom, font, style, text)` — returns int
- Calculates rendered width of text in pixels.
```gs2
temp.width = gettextwidth(1, "Tempus Sans ITC", "b", text);
// Used for centering text
temp.x = thiso.x - gettextwidth(zoom, style, font, text) / 32;
```

### gettextheight
`gettextheight(zoom, font, style, text)` — returns int
- Calculates rendered height of text in pixels.
```gs2
temp.height = gettextheight(1, "Arial", "b", text);

// Also available as profile method (HIDDEN):
temp.height = profile.gettextheight(text);
temp.width = profile.gettextwidth(text);
```

---

## Animation Lookup Functions

### findani
`findani(aniname)` — returns object
- Finds and returns a GANI animation object by name. Allows modifying animation sprite properties.
```gs2
// Change saber color on player's animation sprites
with(findani(player.ani)) {
  getsprite(13).blue = player.client.sabrecolor[0] / 10;
  getsprite(13).red = player.client.sabrecolor[1] / 10;
  getsprite(13).green = player.client.sabrecolor[2] / 10;
  getsprite(13).alpha = player.client.sabrecolor[3];
}
```

### replaceani
`replaceani(originalani, newani)` — returns void
- Replaces a default game animation with a custom one.
```gs2
replaceani("walk", "era_zombie-walk");
replaceani("idle", "era_zombie-walk");
```

---

## Object Existence and Finding Functions

### isobject
`isobject(objectname)` — returns bool
- Checks if a named object (GUI control, variable, etc.) exists.
```gs2
if (isobject("quickfavorites_bgcontrol")) return true;
if (!isobject("Tutorial_Skip_Button")) {
  // Create the button
}
```

### findobject
`findobject(objectname)` — returns object
- Finds a named object in the script universe.
```gs2
temp.obj = findobject("Whiteout_Cell_" @ dx + i @ "_" @ dy + ii);
showSelectedHighlight(findobject("Royale_Item_" @ hotkeynum));
```

### findlevel (Clientside)
`findlevel(filename)` — returns object
- Finds a level object by filename (clientside).

### findnpcbyid (Clientside)
`findnpcbyid(id)` — returns object
- Finds an NPC by its server-side ID (clientside).

---

## Player Visibility and Control Functions

### hideplayer
`hideplayer(seconds)` — returns void
- Hides the player character for the specified duration.
```gs2
hideplayer(0);   // Permanently hide
hideplayer(.5);  // Hide for 0.5 seconds
```

### showplayer
Implicit — player is shown by default. Use `hideplayer(0)` then re-show by setting `player.visible = true` or resetting.

### freezeserverplayer / unfreezeserverplayer (TClient)
`tclient_freezeplayer(state)` — returns void
`tclient_unfreezeplayer(state)` — returns void
- Internal client-level player freeze/unfreeze.

---

## Coordinate Conversion Functions

### screenx / screeny
`screenx(worldx, worldy)` — returns int
`screeny(worldx, worldy)` — returns int
- Converts world (level) coordinates to screen pixel coordinates.
```gs2
temp.screenX = screenx(player.x + 1, player.y - 1.5);
temp.screenY = screeny(player.x + 1, player.y);
showimg(200, "arrow.png", screenX, screenY);

// Circular drawing using coordinate conversion
temp.xValue = screenx(this.x + temp.radius * cos(temp.i), 0);
temp.yValue = screeny(0, this.y - temp.radius * sin(temp.i));
```

### worldx / worldy
`worldx(screenx, screeny)` — returns float
`worldy(screenx, screeny)` — returns float
- Converts screen pixel coordinates to world (level) coordinates.
```gs2
temp.cx0 = worldx(0, 0);
temp.cx1 = worldx(screenwidth, screenheight);
// cx0/cx1 now define the visible area in world coordinates
```

---

## Bomb and Explosive Functions

### explodebomb
`explodebomb(index)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Explodes a bomb by its index in the bombs[] array.

### putleaps
`putleaps(count, x, y)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Places leaping effects (bushes/stones bounce) at position.

### hitobjects
`hitobjects(power, x, y)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Hurts all players, NPCs, and baddies at the specified position.
```gs2
hitobjects(0.5, this.x + 1.5, this.y + 2);
```

---

## Board and Terrain Functions

### updateboard
`updateboard(x, y, width, height)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Makes board/tile modifications visible in the specified area.
```gs2
updateboard(0, 0, 64, 64);  // Update entire 64x64 tile level
```

### updateterrain
`updateterrain()` — returns void (Clientside)
- Makes terrain modifications visible. Call after modifying terrain data.

---

## Player Appearance Customization Functions

### setletters
`setletters(filename)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sets the image used for displaying sign text (replaces default letters.png).
```gs2
setletters("graal2002letters.png");
```

### setspritesimage
`setspritesimage(filename)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Replaces the default sprites image used for NPC rendering.
```gs2
setspritesimage("era_angel_sprites-1.png");
```

### setstatusimage
`setstatusimage(filename)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sets the status image used in the player list/status display.
```gs2
setstatusimage("state.png");
setstatusimage(player.bacongod_options.statusimage);
```

---

## Input and Key Binding Functions

### getkeycode
`getkeycode(keyname)` — returns int (Clientside)
- Gets the numeric key code from a key name string.
```gs2
this.keyPressed.(@ getkeycode("d")) = false;
keydown2(getkeycode("-"), true)
```

### keyname
`keyname(keycode)` — returns string (Clientside)
- Gets the key name string from a numeric key code.
```gs2
temp.keyName = keyname(9);  // Returns "Q" or similar
temp.msg = "Press '" @ keyname(6) @ "'";
```

### keyname2 [HIDDEN]
`keyname2(keycode)` — returns string (Clientside)
- Alternative key name function.

### keydown / keydown2 / keydownglobal / keydown2global
`keydown(keycode)` — returns bool (Clientside)
`keydown2(keycode, ignorecase)` — returns bool (Clientside)
`keydownglobal(keycode)` — returns bool (Clientside)
`keydown2global(keycode, ignorecase)` — returns bool (Clientside)
- Checks if a key is currently held down.
- `keydownglobal`/`keydown2global` work even when game window is not focused.
- Key codes: 0=up, 1=left, 2=down, 3=right, 4=D, 5=S, 6=A, 7=M, 8=Tab, 9=Q, 10=P

### Control Binding (TInput) — Privileged
- `getControlBinding()` — returns object
- `setControlBinding(action, key, modifiers)` — returns void
- `adventure_getcontrolbinding()` — returns object
- `adventure_setcontrolbinding()` — returns void

---

## Server Navigation Functions

### serverwarp
`serverwarp(servername)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Warps the player to a different Graal server.
```gs2
serverwarp("Login");
```

### getservername
`getservername()` — returns string
- Returns the current server name.

### getserverip (Privileged)
`adventure_getserverip()` — returns string
- Returns the current server IP address.

---

## Platform and Environment Functions

### getplatform
`getplatform()` — returns string (Clientside)
- Returns the current platform: `"win"`, `"mac"`, `"iphone"`, `"android"`, `"linux"`.
```gs2
if (getplatform() != "iphone") {
  // PC-only features
}
```

### getloginaccountname
`getloginaccountname()` — returns string (Clientside)
- Returns the current login account name.

### isofflinemode
`isofflinemode()` — returns bool (Clientside)
- Returns true if running in offline/single-player mode.

### isfullscreenmode
`isfullscreenmode()` — returns bool (Clientside)
- Returns true if game is in fullscreen mode.

### isadminguild
`isadminguild(guildname)` — returns bool (Clientside)
- Returns true if the specified guild is an admin/staff guild.

### isclassloaded
`isclassloaded(classname)` — returns bool (Clientside)
- Returns true if the specified class has been loaded.

---

## Stats and Features Control

### showstats
`showstats(bitflag)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Shows/hides parts of the stats bar using bit flags:
  - 1 = ASD display
  - 2 = Icons
  - 4 = Rupees
  - 8 = Bombs
  - 16 = Arrows
  - 32 = Hearts
  - 64 = AP
  - 128 = MP
  - 256 = Minimap
  - 512 = Inventory
  - 1024 = Player
- Combine with addition: `showstats(4 + 8 + 32);` shows rupees, bombs, hearts.
```gs2
showstats(1024);  // Show player in stats
```

### enablefeatures
`enablefeatures(bitflag)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Enables/disables game features using bit flags.
```gs2
enablefeatures(allfeatures);  // Enable all features
```

---

## URL Opening Functions

### openurl
`openurl(url)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Opens a URL in the default web browser. Do NOT include `http://` prefix.
```gs2
openurl(temp.adurl);
openurl("www.graalonline.com");
```

### openurl2
`openurl2(url, x, y)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Opens URL with position parameters.

### opengraalurl
`opengraalurl(url)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Opens a Graal-specific URL (graal:// protocol).

---

## Visual Effect Functions — Extended

### seteffect
`seteffect(red, green, blue, alpha)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sets a color overlay effect on the screen. Values 0.0-1.0.
- Alpha 0 = no effect, 1 = full overlay.
```gs2
seteffect(1, 1, 1, 1);  // Full white overlay
seteffect(0, 0, 0, 0);  // No effect (clear)
```

### seteffectmode
`seteffectmode(mode)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sets the rendering mode for the color effect.
- Modes: 0=transparent, 1=additive, 2=subtract, 3=day/night, 4=custom.
```gs2
seteffectmode(0);  // Transparent mode
seteffectmode(4);  // Custom mode
```

### setfocus
`setfocus(x, y)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sets the camera focus point to world coordinates.

### resetfocus
`resetfocus()` — returns void (Clientside)
- Resets camera focus to default (follows player).
```gs2
resetfocus();
```

---

## File Resource Management

### freefileresources
`freefileresources(filename)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Frees memory used by a loaded file resource (image, sound, etc.).

### freeallresources
`freeallresources()` — returns void (Global)
- Frees all loaded file resources from memory. Use sparingly.

### getscriptaccessfile
`getscriptaccessfile(filename, bool)` — returns string
- Gets file content with script access verification.

### findfiles
`findfiles(pattern, flags)` — returns object
- Finds files matching a glob pattern. Flags: 1=recursive.
```gs2
temp.files = findfiles("levels/*.nw", 0);
```

### getextension
`getextension(filename)` — returns string
- Extracts the file extension from a filename.

### extractfileext
`extractfileext(filename)` — returns string
- Extracts file extension (same as getextension).

### getfilemodtime
`getfilemodtime(filename)` — returns int
- Returns file modification time as Unix timestamp.

---

## GUI Control — Additional Methods

### createanimation
`control.createanimation()` — returns object
- Creates an animation object on a GUI control for smooth property transitions.
```gs2
with (MissionWindow.createanimation()) {
  // Animate window fade-in
  // Property transitions go here
}
```

### setcontentcontrol
`Canvas.setcontentcontrol(control)` — returns void
- Sets the main content control on the Canvas.

### pushdialog / popdialog
`Canvas.pushdialog(control)` — returns void
`Canvas.popdialog()` — returns void
- Pushes/pops a modal dialog on the Canvas.

### sortcontrols
`control.sortcontrols()` — returns void
- Sorts child controls of a container.

### showtop
`control.showtop()` — returns void
- Shows control, brings to front, and tabs to first control.

### showAlwaysTop
`control.showAlwaysTop()` — returns void
- Makes a control always display on top of other windows.

### stopanimations
`control.stopanimations()` — returns void
- Stops all animations on a GUI control.

### stopinoutanimations
`control.stopinoutanimations()` — returns void
- Stops in/out transition animations.

### isactuallyvisible
`control.isactuallyvisible()` — returns bool
- Returns true if control is actually visible (not just `.visible=true`, but parent chain visible too).

### mouseunlockall
`GuiControl.mouseunlockall()` — returns void
- Unlocks mouse from all controls.

---

## Text Edit Control — Additional Methods

### setselection
`textedit.setselection(start, end)` — returns void
- Selects text between character positions.

### undo
`textedit.undo()` — returns void
- Undoes the last text edit operation.

## Chat and Communication Functions

### sendtext
`sendtext(target, message, params...)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sends text to a specified target system.

### requesttext
`requesttext(target, message)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Requests text data from a target system.

### sendtorc
`sendtorc(message)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sends a message to the RC (Remote Control) chat window.

### sendrpgmessage
`sendrpgmessage(message)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sends a message to the player's RPG log window (F2).

### savelog (Clientside)
`savelog(message)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Saves a log message to the server log.

---

## Triggeraction — Complete Documentation
`triggeraction(x, y, action, params...)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Triggers an action at specific tile coordinates. Objects at that position receive the action.
- Actions are received via `onAction{ActionName}()` event handlers.
```gs2
// Trigger a custom action
triggeraction(player.x, player.y, "ChestOpen", "");

// With data parameters
triggeraction(player.x, player.y, "Cluck", {player.dir, player.name});

// Handler in receiving NPC/weapon
function onActionChestOpen(params) {
  this.open();
}
```

---

## Player Functions — Extended Details

### setshield
`setshield(imgname, power)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sets player's shield image and defense power.

### setsword
`setsword(imgname, power)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sets player's sword image and attack power.

### setani
`setani(ganifile, params)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Forces player animation. Second parameter passes GANI parameters.

### Color Setting Functions
- `setskincolor(color)` — Set skin color
- `setcoatcolor(color)` — Set coat/shirt color
- `setsleevecolor(color)` — Set sleeve color
- `setshoecolor(color)` — Set shoe color
- `setbeltcolor(color)` — Set belt color
- Colors are strings: "red", "blue", "green", "orange", "yellow", "purple", "gray", "black", "brown", "pink", "white", "cyan"

---

## NPC Functions — Extended Details

### setshootparams
`setshootparams(params)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Sets parameters passed to projectiles when they hit something.
- First parameter is the action name; remaining parameters are passed to the handler.
```gs2
setshootparams("Cluck", player.account, player.dir, player.name);
```

### say / say2
`say(signindex)` — returns void (Clientside) — Displays a sign by index
`say2(text)` — returns void (Clientside) — Displays text as a sign popup

### lay2 [HIDDEN]
`lay2(itemname, x, y)` — returns void (Clientside)
- Lays an item at specific coordinates.

---

## File System — Privileged Adventure Functions (TFileScripting)

These functions are **privileged** and only available on login servers or special configurations.

### File Operations
- `adventure_fileexists(filename)` — returns bool
- `adventure_fileisfolder(path)` — returns bool
- `adventure_filesize(filename)` — returns int
- `adventure_getfilecontent(filename)` — returns string (reads entire file)
- `adventure_setfilecontent(filename, content)` — returns void (writes entire file)
- `adventure_getapplicationfolder(type)` — returns string (system folder paths)
- `adventure_getfolder(path)` — returns object (directory listing)

### File Time Operations
- `adventure_getfilemodtimeglobal(filename)` — returns int (global mod time)
- `adventure_setfilemodtime(filename, time)` — returns void
- `adventure_setfilemodtimeglobal(filename, time)` — returns void

---

## GUI Profile Methods

### preloadfont [HIDDEN]
`profile.preloadfont()` — returns void
- Preloads the profile's font into memory and keeps it cached until `freeAllResources()` is called.

### gettextwidth / gettextheight (Profile Methods)
`profile.gettextwidth(text)` — returns int
`profile.gettextheight(text)` — returns int
- Gets rendered text dimensions using the profile's font settings.
```gs2
temp.width = profile.gettextwidth(text);
temp.height = profile.gettextheight(text) + 5;
```

---

## Chess Rating System — Extended

### updateratings
`updateratings(ratingsarray)` — returns object
- Updates ELO-style ratings for competitive matchmaking.
- Input format: `{rating1, deviation1, rating2, deviation2, ...}`
- Default starting values: rating=1500, deviation=350.
```gs2
temp.ratings = {1500, 350, 1500, 350};  // Two players
temp.newratings = updateratings(temp.ratings);
```

### updateratingdeviation
`updateratingdeviation(deviation)` — returns float
- Updates rating deviation for a single player based on time elapsed.

---

## Misc Utility Functions

### format2
`format2(formatstring, array)` — returns string
- Formats a string using an array of values (like sprintf with array input).

### getascii
`getascii(character)` — returns int
- Returns ASCII value of a character.

### copystrings
`copystrings(source, destination)` — returns void
- Copies string variables from source to destination.

### getmapx / getmapy
`getmapx(levelfile)` — returns int
`getmapy(levelfile)` — returns int
- Gets the X/Y position of a level on the overworld map.

### findnearestplayer / findnearestplayers
`findnearestplayer(x, y)` — returns object
`findnearestplayers(x, y)` — returns object
- Finds nearest player(s) to the given coordinates.

### getnearestplayer / getnearestplayers
`getnearestplayer(x, y)` — returns int (player index)
`getnearestplayers(x, y)` — returns object
- Gets nearest player index/object(s).

### callnpc
`callnpc(index, params...)` — returns void (Server-side)
- Calls another NPC's script function by index.

---

## Hidden/Privileged Client Functions

These functions exist in the FourPlay source but are marked as `[HIDDEN]` — they don't appear in `listscriptfunctions()` but are still callable.

### Movement Control (Hidden)
- `disabledefmovement()` — Disable default keyboard movement
- `enabledefmovement()` — Enable default keyboard movement
- `disablemap()` — Disable map viewing
- `enablemap()` — Enable map viewing
- `disablepause()` — Disable pause functionality
- `enablepause()` — Enable pause functionality
- `disableselectweapons()` — Disable weapon selection
- `enableselectweapons()` — Enable weapon selection
- `disableweapons()` — Disable weapon firing
- `enableweapons()` — Enable weapon firing
- `noplayerkilling()` — Disable player killing

### Hidden Client Functions
- `attachplayertoobj(objindex, type)` — Attach player to object
- `detachplayer()` — Detach player from object
- `callweapon(index, params...)` — Call weapon script (deprecated)
- `freezeplayer(seconds)` — Freeze player movement
- `hidesword(seconds)` — Hide player's sword
- `hideplayer(seconds)` — Hide player character
- `loadclass(classname)` — Load a class
- `loadmap(mapname)` — Load a map file
- `makescreenshot2(...)` — Advanced screenshot
- `keyname2(keycode)` — Alternative key name
- `lay2(itemname, x, y)` — Lay item at position
- `ischosenofflinemode()` — Check if offline mode selected
- `isvideoplayeropen()` — Check if video player is open
- `openvideoplayer(url, filename)` — Open video player
- `stopvideoplayer()` — Stop video player
- `rungarbagecollector()` — Force garbage collection
- `destroy()` — Destroy object (TStaticVar)

---

## TGraalVar Base Object Methods (Available on ALL Objects)

Every object in GS2 inherits from `TGraalVar`. These methods are available on NPCs, players, weapons, GUI controls, arrays — everything.

### Event Handling

`catchevent(object, eventname, functionname)` — returns void
- Adds an event handler. When `object` fires `eventname`, `functionname` is called on this object. The first parameter received is the source object.
```gs2
thiso.catchevent(this, "onSelect", "onEditVariable");
thiso.catchevent(name, "onAction", "debuggerCall");
```

`ignoreevent(objectname, eventname)` — returns void
- Stops listening for a specific event from a specific object.

`ignoreevents(objectname)` — returns void
- Stops listening for ALL events from the specified object.

`trigger(eventname, params...)` — returns void
- Invokes `onEvent{eventname}()` on this object. Does NOT interrupt the current script.
```gs2
this.trigger("update");
Achievements.trigger("TrackHours");
this.trigger("onResumingCode", "");
```

`scheduleevent(delay, eventname, params...)` — returns object
- Invokes `onEvent{eventname}()` after `delay` seconds.

`cancelevents(eventname)` — returns void
- Removes all previously scheduled events with the specified name.

### Variable Management

`clearvars()` — returns void
- Clears ALL dynamic variables on this object.
```gs2
this.clearvars();
this.achievement.clearvars();
```

`clearemptyvars()` — returns void
- Removes empty subvariables from memory.

`clearvars()` vs `clearemptyvars()`: `clearvars()` wipes everything; `clearemptyvars()` only removes vars with null/empty values.

`getvarnames()` — returns object (array of strings)
- Gets names of ALL subvariables (built-in + dynamic).
```gs2
temp.vars = obj.getvarnames();
```

`getdynamicvarnames()` — returns object
- Gets names of only dynamic (non-built-in) subvariables.

`getstaticvarnames()` — returns object
- Gets names of only built-in (static) subvariables.

`geteditvarnames()` — returns object
- Gets names of dynamic variables that can be modified.

`getfunctions()` — returns object
- Gets all function names as array. Each entry has "parameters", "description", "scope" subvariables.
```gs2
temp.functionz = this.getfunctions();
```

`hasfunction(functionname)` — returns bool
- Checks if this object has a callable function with that name.
```gs2
if (this.hasfunction("destroy")) {
  this.destroy();
}
if (!temp.event.hasfunction("getEventLevel")) {
  return;
}
```

`copyfrom(sourceobject)` — returns void
- Copies all variables and array values from another object. Does not work for protected variables like players.

`objecttype()` — returns string
- Returns object type name: "TGraalVar", "TServerPlayer", "TServerNPC", "TProjectile", etc.

### File I/O (Called on variable objects)

`loadlines(filename)` — loads file into array, line by line
`loadstring(filename)` — loads file content as single string
`savelines(filename, mode)` — saves array to file (0=overwrite, 1=append)
`savestring(filename, mode)` — saves value as string to file
`loadvars(filename)` — loads varname=value pairs from file into subvariables
`savevars(filename, mode)` — saves subvariables as varname=value to file
```gs2
this.options.loadvars("ScriptedRCOptions.txt");
this.options.savevars("ScriptedRCOptions.txt", false);

temp.info.loadvars("apartments/" @ temp.house);
```

`loadfolder(pattern, recursive)` — returns void
- Loads folder contents into variable. Pattern like `"levels/bodies/*.png"`, recursive is 0 or 1.
```gs2
temp.files.loadfolder(temp.file_path, 0);
temp.folder.loadfolder(temp.var @ "/*.nw", true);
```

`loadini(filename)` — returns void
- Loads an .ini file. Access values via `obj.inisection.varname`.

### JSON (Called on variable objects)

`loadjson(filename)` — returns void
- Loads a JSON file into the variable.

`loadjsonfromstring(jsonstring)` — returns void
- Parses a JSON string into the variable structure.
```gs2
temp.eventData.loadjsonfromstring(temp.eventArray);
temp.defaultClass.loadjsonfromstring(temp.data);
```

`savejson(filename, mode, flags)` — returns void
- Saves variable to JSON file. Mode: 0=overwrite, 1=append. Flags: 0=normal, 1=beautify (newlines+indents).

`savejsontostring(flags)` — returns string
- Saves variable to JSON string. Flags: 0=normal, +1=beautify, +2=force strings with comma as array.
```gs2
temp.json = data.savejsontostring(0);
```

`parsejson(variable, jsonstring)` — **DEPRECATED** — Use `loadjsonfromstring()` instead.

### XML (Called on variable objects)

`loadxml(filename)` — loads XML file into array members with names and text attribute
`loadxmlfromstring(xmlstring)` — loads XML string
`savexml(filename, mode)` — saves to XML file
`savexmltostring()` — returns string — saves to XML string

### Array Operations (On variable objects)

`addarray(sourcearray)` — adds another array to this variable
`insertarray(index, array)` — inserts array at specified index
`subarray2(start1, len1, start2, len2)` — returns object — extracts 2D subarray
`addarraymember(name)` — returns object — adds named array member
`getarraymember(name)` — returns object — finds array member by name
`addnamedstring(name, value)` — returns object — adds a named string subvariable
`sortascending()` — sorts array ascending
`sortdescending()` — sorts array descending
`sortbyvalue(varname, type, ascending)` — sorts array by member variable. Type: "string" or float.
`savevarstoarray(sorted)` — returns object — saves subvars as varname=value array
`loadvarsfromarray(array)` — loads from varname=value array

### Other TGraalVar Properties

`name` — string — Object name
`timeout` — float — Timer value (triggers onTimeout when it reaches 0)
`joinedclasses` — object — Classes this object has joined
`maxlooplimit` — integer — Maximum loop iterations (default 10000)
`ispaused` — boolean — Suspends script when set to true
`initialized` — boolean (read-only)
`scripterrors` — object (read-only) — Script errors for this object
`scriptlogmissingfunctions` — boolean

---

## Vector and Matrix Math Functions

All vector/matrix functions are **global** (client + server). Vectors are strings like `"{x, y, z}"`.

### Vector Functions

`vectoradd(v1, v2)` — returns string — `{x1+x2, y1+y2, z1+z2}`
`vectorsub(v1, v2)` — returns string — `{x1-x2, y1-y2, z1-z2}`
`vectorscale(v, scalar)` — returns string — `{x*s, y*s, z*s}`
`vectordot(v1, v2)` — returns float — Dot product
`vectorcross(v1, v2)` — returns string — Cross product vector
`vectorlen(v)` — returns float — Vector magnitude
`vectornormalize(v)` — returns string — Unit vector (length 1)
`vectordist(v1, v2)` — returns float — Distance between two points
```gs2
temp.dist = vectordist({mousex, mousey, 0}, this.last_click);
// Returns Euclidean distance
```
`vectororthobasis(v)` — returns string — Orthogonal basis vectors

### Matrix Functions

`matrixcreate(position, rotation)` — returns string — Creates transform matrix from position `{x,y,z}` and rotation `{x,y,z,angle}`
`matrixcreatefromeuler(eulerangles)` — returns string — Creates matrix from Euler angles `{x,y,z}`
`matrixmultiply(m1, m2)` — returns string — Multiplies two matrices
`matrixmulpoint(matrix, point)` — returns string — Transforms a point by matrix
`matrixmulvector(matrix, vector)` — returns string — Transforms a vector by matrix

### Rotation Functions

`rotationadd(r1, r2)` — returns string — Adds two rotations
`rotationsub(r1, r2)` — returns string — Subtracts rotations
`rotationaddeuler(rotation, euler)` — returns string — Adds Euler rotation
`rotationfromeuler(euler)` — returns string — Converts Euler to rotation
`rotationtoeuler(rotation)` — returns string — Converts rotation to Euler

### Box Functions

`boxcontains(box, point)` — returns bool — Is point inside box?
`boxcontainsvector(box, vector)` — returns bool — Is vector inside box?
`boxoverlaps(box1, box2)` — returns bool — Do boxes overlap?
`boxintersect(box1, box2)` — returns string — Returns intersection box
`boxscale(box, scale)` — returns string — Scales a box
`getboxcenter(box)` — returns string — Returns center point of box

### Ray Casting

`castray(start, end, objecttypes)` — returns object — Casts a ray from start to end, returns first hit. Object types: `"staticts,terrain,interior"`

---

## UTF-8 and Text Encoding Functions

All **global** (client + server).

`convertlatintoutf8(string)` — returns string — Converts Latin-1 to UTF-8
`convertutf8tolatin(string)` — returns string — Converts UTF-8 to Latin-1
`isstringutf8(string)` — returns bool — Checks if string is valid UTF-8
`addutf8fontrange(fontfile, startpage, endpage)` — returns void — Registers a font for specific Unicode code pages
`clearutf8fontranges()` — returns void — Clears all UTF-8 font ranges

## TServerLevel — Complete Method Reference

All methods are called on `level` object or objects returned by `findlevel()`.

### Bomb/Explosion
`level.putbomb(power, x, y)` — Place bomb
`level.putbomb2(power, x, y, image)` — Place bomb with custom image (default bomb1.png)
`level.putexplosion(power, x, y)` — Place explosion
`level.putexplosion2(power1, power2, x, y)` — Place explosion with two power values
`level.removebomb(index)` — Remove bomb by index
`level.removeexplo(index)` — Remove explosion by index
`level.testbomb(x, y)` — returns int — Test if bomb at position
`level.testexplo(x, y)` — returns int — Test if explosion at position

### Items and Objects
`level.removeitem(index)` — Remove item by index
`level.testitem(x, y)` — returns int — Test if item at position
`level.puthorse(image, x, y)` — Place horse
`level.removehorse(index)` — Remove horse
`level.testhorse(x, y)` — returns int — Test if horse at position
`level.reflectarrow(index)` — Reflect arrow
`level.removearrow(index)` — Remove arrow

### NPC and Player Testing
`level.testnpc(x, y)` — returns int — Get NPC index at position
`level.testsign(x, y)` — returns int — Get sign index at position
`level.findareanpcs(x, y, width, height)` — returns object — Find NPCs in rectangle
`level.hitcompu(id, power, x, y)` — Hit computer opponent
`level.removecompus()` — Remove all computer opponents

### Projectile
`level.shoot(x, y, z, angle, zangle, strength, ani, aniparams)` — Shoot projectile

### Tiles and Height
`level.tiletype(x, y)` — returns int — Get tile type at position
`level.setz(x, y, w, h, z1, z2, z3, z4)` — Set Z heights for a rectangle (corner heights)
`level.getmappartfile(x, y)` — returns string — Get map part filename at position

## TServerNPC — Complete Method Reference

### Visibility and Blocking
`npc.hide()` / `npc.show()` — Hide/show NPC
`npc.hidelocal()` / `npc.showlocal()` — Hide/show locally (client-side only)
`npc.blockagain()` / `npc.dontblock()` — Enable/disable blocking
`npc.blockagainlocal()` / `npc.dontblocklocal()` — Local blocking control
`npc.drawoverplayer()` / `npc.drawunderplayer()` — Drawing order
`npc.drawaslight()` — Draw as light source
`npc.timereverywhere()` — Timer runs even when no players in level

### Carrying
`npc.carryobject(objectname)` — Start carrying object
`npc.throwcarry()` — Throw carried object
`npc.take2(index)` — Take item by index
`npc.takehorse(index)` — Take horse

### Appearance
`npc.setbow(image)` — Set bow image
`npc.setchargender(gender)` — Set character gender
`npc.setzoomeffect(zoomfactor)` — Zoom (>1) or shrink (<1) the NPC
`npc.setshape(type, width, height)` — Set rectangular collision box. Type 1=rectangular.
`npc.setshape2(tilewidth, tileheight, tiletypes)` — Set tile-based collision

### Image Display (Extended)
`npc.showani2(index, x, y, z, direction, animation)` — returns object — Show animation with Z
`npc.showtext2(index, x, y, z, font, style, text)` — returns object — Show text with Z
`npc.showpoly2(index, coordinates)` — returns object — Show polygon variant

## TPlayer — Complete Method Reference (Client-Side)

### Combat
`player.getnohit(checkstring)` — returns int — Checks if player can be hurt. Returns error code (0 = can be hurt).

### Emoticons
`player.showemoticon(emoticonfile)` — Show an emoticon
`player.showemoticonbykey(keycode)` — Show emoticon by key (e.g., VK_A)
`player.hideemoticon()` — Hide current emoticon

### Signs
`player.hidesign()` — Stop reading a sign if currently reading
`player.scrollsign(keyaction)` — Scroll sign page. 0=up, others=down

### Profile and PM
`player.showprofile()` — Show player profile window
`player.isguildpm()` — returns bool — Is current PM a guild PM?
`player.ismasspm()` — returns bool — Is current PM a mass PM?
`player.pmswaiting()` — returns bool — Are there PMs waiting?
`player.openexternalpm(showsent)` — returns bool — Open external PM window. true = show sent PMs
`player.openexternalhistory()` — returns bool — Open PM history window

### Weapons
`player.findweapon(weaponname)` — returns object — Find weapon by name

## Static Objects (Array, Math, Key, Date, Color, Sound)

These are built-in object types accessible as `new Array()`, `Math.function()`, etc.

### Array Object
```gs2
temp.arr = new Array();
temp.arr.push("item1", "item2");  // Add to end, returns new length
temp.len = temp.arr.length;        // Get length (read-only)
temp.arr.splice(0, 2);             // Remove 2 elements starting at index 0
temp.arr.unshift("first");         // Add to beginning, returns new length
temp.arr.array(val1, val2, ...);   // Constructor-like creation
```

### Math Object
```gs2
Math.PI           // 3.14159265...
Math.SQRT2        // 1.41421...
Math.sqrt1_2      // 0.7071...
Math.abs(value)   // Absolute value
Math.ceil(value)  // Round up
Math.floor(value) // Round down (returns int)
Math.round(value) // Round to nearest
Math.sin(angle)   // Sine
Math.cos(angle)   // Cosine
Math.tan(angle)   // Tangent
Math.atan(value)  // Arctangent
Math.atan2(y, x)  // Arctangent of y/x (two params)
Math.log(value)   // Natural logarithm
Math.pow(base, exp) // Power
Math.max(a, b)    // Maximum
Math.min(a, b)    // Minimum
Math.random()     // Random float 0.0-1.0
Math.sqrt(value)  // Square root
```

### Key Object
```gs2
// Constants (all read-only integers)
Key.backspace, Key.capslock, Key.control, Key.deletekey
Key.down, Key.end, Key.enter, Key.escape
Key.home, Key.insert, Key.left, Key.pgdn, Key.pgup
Key.right, Key.shift, Key.space, Key.tab, Key.up

// Methods
Key.isdown(keycode)         // returns bool — Is key held down?
Key.istoggled(keycode)      // returns bool — Is key toggled (CapsLock etc.)?
Key.isaccessible()          // returns bool — Is key input accessible?
Key.getascii()              // returns int — Last ASCII code pressed
Key.getcode()               // returns int — Last keycode pressed
Key.addlistener(object)     // Add key event listener
Key.removelistener(object)  // returns bool — Remove key event listener
```

### Date Object
```gs2
temp.d = new Date();  // or: temp.d = new Date(timestamp);
// Access: d.year, d.month, d.day, d.hour, d.minute, d.second, d.weekday
```

### Color Object
```gs2
temp.c = new Color();
temp.c = new Color(r, g, b);
temp.c.setrgb(rgbvalue);  // Set color from integer
```

### Sound Object
```gs2
temp.s = new Sound();
temp.s = new Sound("effect.wav");
```

---

## GUI Control — Complete Method Reference

### GuiControl (Base — All Controls Inherit These)

**Properties:**
`x`, `y`, `width`, `height` — Position and size (integers)
`visible` — boolean — Visibility
`active` — boolean — Is control active
`alpha` — float — Transparency (0.0-1.0)
`red`, `green`, `blue` — float — Color tint (0.0-1.0)
`mode` — int — Drawing mode (0=add, 1=transparent, 2=subtract, 3=daynight)
`profile` — object — GUI profile
`parent` — object (read-only) — Parent control
`extent` — string — "{width, height}"
`position` — string — "{x, y}"
`clientwidth`, `clientheight` — int — Interior dimensions
`horizsizing` — string — "right", "width", "left", "center", "relative"
`vertsizing` — string — "bottom", "height", "top", "center", "relative"
`canmove`, `canresize` — boolean
`clipchildren` — boolean — Clip children to bounds (default true)
`cliptobounds` — boolean — Clip this control to bounds (default true)
`flickering` — boolean — Auto show/hide flicker
`flickertime` — float — Flicker interval in seconds
`hint` — string — Tooltip text
`showhint` — boolean — Show tooltip
`rotation` — float — Rotation angle
`rotationcenter` — string — "{x, y}" rotation center
`cursor` — string — Cursor type: "default", "hand", "text", "crosshair", "drag", "wait", "help"
`lockmousedown` — boolean — Lock mouse on click (like a button)
`bitmapcache` — boolean — Cache control as bitmap for speed

**Methods:**
`control.addcontrol(child)` — Add child control
`control.clearcontrols()` — Remove all children
`control.bringtofront()` — Bring to front of siblings
`control.pushtoback()` — Send to back of siblings
`control.findcontrol("{x,y}")` — returns object — Find child at point
`control.getparent()` — returns object
`control.destroy()` — Destroy control
`control.show()` / `control.hide()` — Show/hide
`control.showtop()` — Show + bring to front + tab first
`control.resize(x, y, w, h)` — Move and resize
`control.repaint()` — Force repaint
`control.createanimation()` — returns object — Create animation for position/rotation/color transitions
`control.stopanimations()` — Stop all animations
`control.stopinoutanimations()` — Stop in/out transition animations
`control.sortcontrols()` — Sort children by Y coordinate
`control.tabfirst()` — returns object — Focus first tabbable control
`control.startdrag()` / `control.stopdrag()` — Drag control
`control.isactuallyvisible()` — returns bool — Is actually visible (checking parent chain)
`control.isfirstresponder()` — returns bool — Has keyboard focus
`control.makefirstresponder(bool)` — Set keyboard focus
`control.mouselock(mouseid)` — Lock mouse to control (usually 0)
`control.mouseunlock(mouseid)` — Unlock mouse
`control.mouseunlockall()` — Unlock all mice (multitouch)
`control.ismouselocked(mouseid)` — returns bool — Is mouse locked?
`control.globaltolocalcoord("{x,y}")` — returns string — Screen→local coordinates
`control.localtoglobalcoord("{x,y}")` — returns string — Local→screen coordinates

**Events:**
`onAction()` — Button pressed / action occurred
`onAdd()` — Added as child to parent
`onRemove()` — Removed from parent
`onWake()` — Added to screen
`onSleep()` — No longer on screen
`onShow()` — Became visible
`onHide()` — No longer visible
`onMove(x, y)` — Moved
`onResize(width, height)` — Resized
`onDialogPush()` — Became dialog control
`onDialogPop()` — No longer dialog control
`onStyleChanges()` — GUI style changed
`onStartDrag()` — User started dragging
`onStopDrag(mod, x, y, count)` — Stopped dragging
`onBecomeFirstResponder()` — Gained keyboard focus
`onLoseFirstResponder()` — Lost keyboard focus

**Mouse Events** (all: `mod, x, y, clickcount`):
`onMouseDown(mod, x, y, count)`
`onMouseUp(mod, x, y, count)`
`onMouseDragged(mod, x, y, count)`
`onMouseMove(mod, x, y, count)`
`onMouseEnter(mod, x, y, count)`
`onMouseLeave(mod, x, y, count)`
`onMouseWheelUp(mod, x, y, count)`
`onMouseWheelDown(mod, x, y, count)`
`onRightMouseDown(mod, x, y, count)`
`onRightMouseUp(mod, x, y, count)`
`onRightMouseDragged(mod, x, y, count)`

**Key Events:**
`onKeyDown(keycode, keytext, scancode)`
`onKeyUp(keycode, keytext, scancode)`

**File Events:**
`onDropFiles(x, y, filelist)` — Files dropped (requires `acceptdropfiles = true`)

### GuiWindowCtrl (extends GuiTextCtrl)

**Properties:** `canclose`, `canmaximize`, `canminimize`, `canmove`, `canresize`, `closequery`, `destroyonhide`, `clientrelative`, `maximized`, `minimized`, `tile`, `isexternal`, `externalwindow` (read-only)
**Methods:** `isactivewindow()` — returns bool
**Events:** `onCloseQuery()` — Close button clicked (when closequery=true)

### GuiTextListCtrl / GuiTextListEntry

**GuiTextListCtrl Properties:** `columns` ("{0,16}"), `enumerate`, `fitparentwidth`, `selectedid`, `selectedrow`, `sortcolumn`, `sortmode` ("value"/"name"/"extension"), `sortorder`

**GuiTextListCtrl Methods:**
`addrow(id, text)` — returns object (the row)
`insertrow(position, id, text)` — returns object
`removerow(rowindex)` — Remove by index
`removerowbyid(rowid)` — Remove by ID
`clearrows()` — Remove all rows
`clearselection()` — Clear selection
`rowcount()` — returns int
`findtext(text)` — returns int (row index)
`findtextid(text)` — returns int (row id)
`getrowatpoint(x, y)` — returns int (row index at point)
`getrowidatpoint(x, y)` — returns int (row id at point)
`getrownumbyid(id)` — returns int
`getselectedid()` — returns int
`getselectedids()` — returns object (array of ids)
`getselectedrow()` — returns int
`getselectedrows()` — returns object (array of indices)
`getselectedtext()` — returns string
`isidselected(id)` — returns bool
`isrowselected(row)` — returns bool
`makevisible(row)` — Scroll to make row visible
`makevisiblebyid(id)` — Scroll to make row visible by id
`seticonsize(w, h)` — Set icon dimensions
`setrowactivebyid(id, active)` — Enable/disable row
`setrowbyid(id, text)` — Set row text by ID
`setselectedbyid(id)` — Select row by ID
`setselectedbyids(ids)` — Select multiple by IDs (requires allowmultipleselections)
`setselectedbytext(text)` — Select row by text match
`setselectedrow(row)` — Select by index
`setselectedrows(rows)` — Select multiple by indices
`sort()` — Sort rows

**GuiTextListCtrl Events:**
`onSelect(id, text, row)` — Row selected
`onDblClick(id, text, row)` — Double-clicked
`onDeleteKey(id, text, row)` — Delete key on entry
`onOpenMenu(id, text, row)` — Right-click on entry
`onMouseOverCell(id, text, row)` — Mouse hovering over entry
`onIconResized(width, height)` — Icon size changed

**GuiTextListEntry Properties:** `active`, `id`, `text`, `image`, `selectedimage`, `sortgroup`, `sortvalue`, `hint`, `flickering`, `flickertime`
**GuiTextListEntry Methods:** `gettext()` — returns string, `settext(str)` — returns void

`drawline(x1, y1, x2, y2, linewidth)` — Draw line with profile.fontcolor

`saveimage(filename)` — Save canvas to image file
`saveimage2(filename, bitdepth)` — Save with bitdepth (24 or 32)
`saveimagetostring(filename)` — returns string — Raw image data as string
`saveimagetostring2(filename, bitdepth)` — returns string
`setdrawpalette(colors, transparentindex)` — Set color palette

## TSocket — Complete Reference

### Properties
`isconnected` — bool (read-only)
`address` — string (read-only)
`port` — int (read-only)
`data` — string — Incoming data buffer
`error` — string (read-only)
`ipaddress` — string (read-only)
`packagedelimiter` — string — Auto-split incoming data into packages
`parent` — object (read-only) — Server socket this client belongs to
`enablessl` — boolean — Enable SSL before connect()
`sslcertfile` — string — SSL certificate
`sslkeyfile` — string — SSL key
`sslcipherlist` — string — e.g. "AES-128-MD5"
`sslprotocol` — string — e.g. "SSLv3", "TLSv1"
`sslverifycert` — string — Verification certificate
`outdatalength` — int (read-only) — Unsent data amount

### Methods
`socket.connect(host, port)` — Connect to host
`socket.bind(port, isUdp)` — Bind port (isUdp=true for UDP)
`socket.close()` — Close/disconnect/unbind
`socket.send(data)` — Send TCP data
`socket.sendudp(data, ip, port)` — Send UDP data to IP

### Events
`onConnect()` — Connection established
`onConnectFailed()` — Connection failed
`onClose()` — Connection closed
`onBind()` — Bind successful
`onBindFailed()` — Bind failed
`onNewClient(newsocket)` — New client connected (on server socket)
`onReceiveData(data)` — Received TCP data
`onReceiveDataPackage(package)` — Received delimited data package
`onReceiveUDPData(data, senderIP, senderPort)` — Received UDP data

### TRTMPClient (extends TSocket)
`rtmp.createstream(streamid)` — returns object
`rtmp.getstream(streamid)` — returns object
`rtmp.call(channelid, streamid, params...)` — Remote call
`rtmp.sendhandshake()` — Send handshake
`rtmp.sendinitialping()` — Send initial ping

**Events:** `onCall(channelid, streamid, params...)`, `onGeneratedThumbnail(stream, filename)`, `onHandShake()`

---

## Audio Functions — Complete Reference (TSounds)

All **clientside**.

### Sound Effects
`play(soundfile)` — Play at player position
`play2(soundfile, x, y, volume)` — Play at position with volume (0.0-1.0)
`play3d(soundfile, looped, position, velocity)` — 3D positioned sound. Position/velocity: "{x,y,z}"
`playlooped(soundfile)` — Play looped continuously
`playlooped2(soundfile, x, y, volume)` — Play looped at position with volume
`stopsound(soundfile)` — Stop specific sound
`stopsounds(includeLooped)` — Stop all sounds. Pass true to also stop loops
`issoundplaying(soundfile)` — returns bool
`setsoundpitch(soundfile, pitch)` — Set pitch (1.0=normal, 0.5-1.5 typical range)
`setsoundpitchbynote(soundfile, basenote, targetnote)` — Set pitch by musical note. Notes: "an0", "cn1", "fs1", etc.

### Music
`stopmusic()` — Stop current music
`stopmidi()` — Same as stopmusic() (deprecated alias)
`setmusicvolume(volume, fade)` — Set volume (0.0-1.0) with optional fade duration
`getmusicfilename()` — returns string — Currently playing music
`getmusicstatus()` — returns string — Playback status
`getmusictags()` — returns object — Music metadata
`ismusicplaying()` — returns bool

---

## World Rendering and Environment Functions

All **clientside**.

### Day/Night and Visual Effects
`seteffect(r, g, b, a)` — Set color overlay (values 0.0-1.0)
`seteffectmode(mode)` — 0=transparent, 1=additive, 2=subtract, 3=day/night, 4=custom
`setcoloreffect(r, g, b, a)` — Set object color/alpha blending
`setfog_colors(colors)` — Set fog colors array
`setskybandcolors(colors)` — Set sky band colors
`setskybandsizes(sizes)` — Set sky band sizes
`setsuncolors(colors)` — Set sun colors
`setinteriorrendermode(mode)` — Set interior rendering mode
`setterrainrendermode(mode)` — Set terrain rendering mode
`lightscene()` — returns bool — Recalculate scene lighting
`updatevisibledistance()` — Update visible distance

### Minimap
`setminimap(imagefile, levellistfile, defaultx, defaulty)` — Set minimap. Image is the map picture, levellistfile maps levels to positions.

### Video Recording
`startrecordvideo()` — returns bool — Start video recording
`stoprecordvideo()` — returns bool — Stop video recording

### Graphics Control
`switchtodirectx()` — Switch to DirectX renderer
`switchtoopengl()` — Switch to OpenGL renderer
`supportsopenglshader()` — returns bool — Does system support GLSL shaders
`setcursor2(cursorname)` — Set cursor type
`setstreamingmode(mode)` — returns bool — Set streaming mode
`synctimeofday(time, speed)` — Synchronize world day/night cycle
`update3dterrain(x, y, w, h)` — Update 3D terrain area
`graalcontrolhasfocus(includeChat)` — returns bool — Does Graal control have focus

### Platform Info
`getplatform()` — returns string — "win", "mac", "iphone", "android", "linux"
`getdesktopresolution()` — returns string — Desktop resolution
`getdisplaydevicelist()` — returns object — Available display devices (OpenGL, DirectX9)
`getresolutionlist()` — returns object — Available fullscreen resolutions
`getgamesubversion()` — returns string — Distribution platform ("amazon", "googleplay", etc.)
`isdemoversion()` — returns bool
`ispremiumversion()` — returns bool
`getpremiumoption()` — returns string

### Update Packages
`getbasepackage()` — returns object — Base update package
`getupdatepackage(name)` — returns object — Get update package by name
`getdownloadingpackage()` — returns object — Currently downloading package
`getdownloadingpackagescount()` — returns int
`getdownloadedupdatepackagesize()` — returns int — Bytes downloaded
`gettotalupdatepackagesize()` — returns int — Total bytes to download

### File Operations (Clientside)
`decompressfile(filename, pattern, destination, flags)` — returns bool — Unzip file. Flags: 1=keep paths, 2=keep mod time
`downloadfiletofolder(filename, destination)` — Download file from RC to destination
`selectfilefordownload(dialog)` — Open file dialog for download location
`selectfileforupload()` — Open file dialog for uploading
`requestfiledeletion(filename)` — Request file deletion
`requestfilerename(oldname, newname)` — Request file rename
`requestfilesmove(source, destination)` — Request moving files

### Misc Clientside
`getplayerlisticons()` — returns object — Player list icon array
`gett9words(input)` — returns object — Get T9 word suggestions
`loadt9words(filename)` — Load T9 word dictionary
`ltmfs(bool)` — Unknown (tile map related)
`ltmmax(float, float)` — Tile map max
`ltmmin(float, float)` — Tile map min

## TTileMap System

For new-format levels (.gxml). These are on tilemap objects.

`tilemap.loadtilemap(filename, format)` — returns object — **Deprecated, use loadtilemapxml()**
`tilemap.loadtilemapxml(filename)` — returns object — Load new-format level data
`tilemap.reload()` — Reload tile map
`tilemap.saveasimage(filename, pixelsperunit, clip)` — Generate map screenshot
`tilemap.findanimation(name)` — returns object
`tilemap.findimagemap(name)` — returns object
`tilemap.clear()` — Clear (script-created maps only)
`tilemap.destroy()` — Delete (script-created maps only)

### TTileLayer (inside TTileMap)

`layer.gettileflags(x, y)` — returns string
`layer.gettileframe(x, y)` — returns int
`layer.gettileimagemap(x, y)` — returns string

## TGraalAni (GANI Animation Object)

Accessed via `findani(name)`.

`ani.addsprite(index)` — returns object — Add sprite
`ani.getsprite(index)` — returns object — Get sprite by index
`ani.deletesprite(index)` — Delete sprite
`ani.addstep()` — returns object — Add animation step
`ani.insertstep(index)` — returns object — Insert step at index
`ani.deletestep(index)` — Delete step

### TGraalAniSprite (returned by getsprite)

`sprite.insertattachment(index)` — returns object
`sprite.deleteattachment(index)` — Delete attachment

### TGraalAniStep (returned by addstep/insertstep)

`step.insertpos(index, value)` — returns object — Insert position

## TParticleModifier

`modifier.addmod(type, operation, value1, value2)` — Add sub-modifier

### Particle Modifier Add Functions (on emitter)
`emitter.addlocalmodifier(selectiontype, timestart, timeend, property, operation, valuestart, valueend)` — returns object
`emitter.addglobalmodifier(...)` — returns object
`emitter.addemitmodifier(...)` — returns object

**Selection types:** "once", "range", "loop"
**Operations:** "replace", "add", "multiply"
**Properties:** "x", "y", "z", "alpha", "red", "green", "blue", "zoom", "rotation", "angle", "zangle", "speed", "spin"

---

## MovieClip (ActionScript/Flash Compatibility Layer)

Full ActionScript compatibility for Flash animations embedded in Graal.

### Frame Control
`gotoandplay(frame)` — Go to frame and play
`gotoandstop(frame)` — Go to frame and stop
`nextframe()` — Next frame
`prevframe()` — Previous frame
`play()` — Play animation
`stop()` — Stop animation

### Display Object Management
`attachmovie(sourceclip, name, layer)` — returns object
`createemptymovieclip(name, layer)` — returns object
`duplicatemovieclip(params)` — returns object
`removemovieclip()` — Remove this clip
`unloadmovie()` — Same as removemovieclip()
`swapdepths(layer)` — Swap depth with another clip
`setmask(maskclip)` — Set mask

### Properties
`_x`, `_y` — float — Position
`_xscale`, `_yscale` — float — Scale
`_rotation` — float — Rotation
`_visible` — boolean — Visibility
`_alpha` — float — Alpha (0-100)
`_xmouse`, `_ymouse` — float (read-only) — Mouse position relative to clip
`_name`, `_parent`, `_root`, `_level0` — Object hierarchy

### Methods
`hittest(params)` — returns bool — Hit test
`getbounds(target)` — returns object
`getbytesloaded()` — returns int
`getbytestotal()` — returns int
`getdepth()` — returns int
`getinstanceatdepth(depth)` — returns object
`getlayer(name)` — returns object
`getnexthighestdepth()` — returns int
`getcolor()` — returns string
`setcolor(color)` — Set color
`getproperty(index)` — returns string
`setproperty(index, value)`
`gettimer()` — returns int — Global timer in ms
`startdrag()` / `stopdrag()` — Drag operations
`globaltolocal(obj)` / `localtoglobal(obj)` — Coordinate conversion
`setinterval(object, method, interval)` — returns object — Repeating timer
`clearinterval(intervalobj)` — Clear interval
`updateafterevent()` — Force display update
`removelayer(name)` — Remove layer

### Events
`onEnterFrame()` — Frame tick
`onInitFrame()` — Switched to new frame
`onKeyDown()` / `onKeyUp()`
`onMouseDown()` / `onMouseUp()` / `onMouseMove()`
`onPress()` — Click on button clip
`onRelease()` — Release on button clip

---

## Additional Utility Functions

### String Extraction
`extractfilebase(filename)` — returns string — Filename without path or extension
`extractfilename(filename)` — returns string — Filename with extension, no path
`extractfilepath(filename)` — returns string — Directory path only
`extractfileext(filename)` — returns string — Extension only
`getextension(filename)` — returns string — Same as extractfileext
`escapefilename(filename)` — returns string — Escape special characters
`escapestringkeepnewline(string)` — returns string — Escape but keep newlines

### Searching
`contains(string, substring)` — returns bool — Does string contain substring?
`aindexof(value, array)` — returns int — Index of value in array

### Debugging
`getcallstack()` — returns object — Array of TCallStackEntry objects
`trace(text)` — Same as echo()
`iscursoron()` — returns bool — Is cursor visible?
`cursoron()` / `cursoroff()` — Show/hide cursor

### Terrain Info
`getterraintexture(x, y)` — returns string — Terrain texture at position
`getterraintextureindex(x, y)` — returns int — Terrain texture index

### TSocket Connection
`adddnsentry(hostname, ip)` — returns void — Add custom DNS entry
`resolvehost(hostname)` — returns string — DNS lookup to IPv4

### Terraformer
`loadgrayscale(index, filename)` — returns bool — Load grayscale image
`loadgreyscale(index, filename)` — returns bool — Same as loadgrayscale
`savegreyscale(index, filename)` — returns bool — Save grayscale
`setterrain(index)` — returns bool — Set terrain from grayscale

### GuiTilesCtrl
`loadtilemap(filename, format)` — returns object
`loadtilemapxml(filename)` — returns object

---

## Remaining Edge-Case Functions

### Player Finding — Alias Functions

`findplayerbycommunityname(name)` — returns object — Same as `findplayer2()`. Finds player by account or community name.
`findplayerbyid(id)` — returns object — Finds player by server-assigned integer ID.
```gs2
temp.pl = findplayerbycommunityname("Stefan");
temp.pl = findplayerbyid(5);
```

### HTTP — Alias Function

`gethttprequestforurl(url)` — returns object — Finds an active HTTP request object matching the given URL. Similar to `gethttprequest()` but takes only a URL string.

### Player Carry/Horse — Client-Side

`takeplayercarry()` — returns void (Clientside) — Takes/removes the player's carried object.
`takeplayerhorse()` — returns void (Clientside) — Takes/removes the player's horse.

These are the clientside equivalents. Serverside uses `player.takeplayercarry()` or operates on the NPC directly.

### NPC Showtext (TServerNPC)

`npc.showtext(index, x, y, font, style, text)` — returns object — Displays text on the NPC layer. This is the TServerNPC method form of the global `showtext()`.
```gs2
temp.img = this.showtext(200, this.x + 1.7, this.y - 3.8, "Arial", "cb", "Hello");
```

### NPC Timershow

`timershow()` — returns void (TGraalVar) — **DEPRECATED.** Lets an NPC visually display its current timeout value. No longer used.

### Animation Events (GuiAnimationCtrl)

`onAnimationStart(currentindex, destinationindex)` — Event fired when an animated control (GuiAnimationCtrl, GuiAccordionCtrl, GuiSlideShowCtrl) starts moving to a new entry.
`onAnimationEnd(newindex)` — Event fired when the animated control finishes moving to the new entry.
`onAnimationFinished(transitiontype)` — Event fired on GuiControl when all `createanimation()` transitions complete. Parameter is the last transition type string.

```gs2
// GuiAccordionCtrl or GuiSlideShowCtrl
function onAnimationStart(current, dest) {
  echo("Sliding from " @ current @ " to " @ dest);
}
function onAnimationEnd(newrow) {
  echo("Finished at row " @ newrow);
}

// GuiControl animation completion
function onAnimationFinished(type) {
  echo("All animations done, last type: " @ type);
}
```

### Text Changed Events

`onTextChanged(text)` — Event on GuiTextCtrl, GuiWindowCtrl, GuiStartMenuCtrl — Fires when the text/title changes.
```gs2
function onTextChanged(newtext) {
  echo("Window title changed to: " @ newtext);
}
```

### PM Controls (GuiPMCtrl, GuiPMEditCtrl, GuiPMHistoryCtrl)

`pmctrl.addpm(playerobject)` — Add a received PM from the specified player to the control's text.
`pmctrl.showpm(playerobject)` — Replace the control's text with the PM from this player.
`pmctrl.clear()` — Clear all PM text.
`pmhistory.showhistory(playerobject)` — Show PM history for the specified player.
`pmedit.sendpm(playerobject)` — Send a PM to the specified player.
`pmedit.sendmasspm(playerobject)` — Send a mass PM.
`pmedit.clear()` — Clear the PM edit field.

### Fog Colors

`setfog_colors(colors)` — returns void (Clientside) — Sets fog color array for 3D rendering. Pass an object/array of color values.

Note: This is the same function as `setfogcolors()` — both forms may appear in documentation. The underscore variant is the canonical name.

### Static Object Constructors

`string(value)` — returns object — Constructor for `new String(value)`. Creates a String object.
`color(r, g, b)` — returns object — Constructor for `new Color(r, g, b)`. Creates a Color object.
`date(timestamp)` — returns object — Constructor for `new Date(timestamp)`. Creates a Date object.
`sound(filename)` — returns object — Constructor for `new Sound(filename)`. Creates a Sound object.
`stage(params)` — returns object — Constructor for Stage object (ActionScript/Flash compatibility, accesses the Flash stage).
```gs2
temp.myString = new String("hello");
temp.myColor = new Color(255, 0, 0);
temp.myDate = new Date(timevar2);
temp.mySound = new Sound("effect.wav");
```

### THTTPRequest — URL Lookup

`gethttprequestforurl(url)` — returns object — Finds an active HTTP request by full URL string. Returns the THTTPRequest object if found, null otherwise.

### Vector Conversion Functions

`getanglesfromvector(vector)` — returns object — Converts a direction vector `{x,y,z}` to angles. Returns `{yaw, pitch}`.
`getvectorfromangles(yaw, pitch)` — returns string — Converts angles to a direction vector `{x,y,z}`. Inverse of `getanglesfromvector()`.
```gs2
// Convert direction to angles
temp.angles = getanglesfromvector("{0.5, 0.5, 0}");
// Convert angles back to vector
temp.vec = getvectorfromangles(temp.angles[0], temp.angles[1]);
```

### Material Mapping (3D)

`addmaterialmapping(materialname, texturefile)` — returns bool — Adds a material texture mapping for 3D shape rendering.

### GANI Sprite Lookup

`ani.getimageforsprite(spriteobject)` — returns string — Gets the image filename used by a given sprite in a GANI animation.
`ani.getspriteforspritepos(positionobject)` — returns object — Gets the sprite object at a specific sprite position in a GANI.

### GameMovementInterpolate / GameMovementRigid (3D Physics)

`interpolate.applyimpulse(position, force)` — Apply a physical impulse at position with force vector.
`rigid.addrigidmove(torque, force)` — Adds torque and force vectors for rigid body movement.
`movementlist.move3d(direction, speed, flags)` — Queue a 3D movement.
`movementlist.finishmoves()` — Finish all queued movements.

### GameMovementList (3D Movement Queue)

`movementlist.move3d(direction, speed, flags)` — Queue 3D movement with direction vector, speed, and flags.
`movementlist.finishmoves()` — Process and finish all queued 3D movements.

### GameObject — Mounting System (3D)

`obj.getcontrollingobject()` — returns object — Gets the object controlling this object.
`obj.getcontrollingclient()` — returns object — Gets the client (player) controlling this object.
`obj.getinteriorobject()` — returns object — Gets the interior object this object is inside.
`obj.getmountedobject(index)` — returns object — Gets mounted object at index.
`obj.getmountedobjectcount()` — returns int — Number of mounted objects.
`obj.getmountedobjectnode(index)` — returns int — Gets the mount node for mounted object at index.
`obj.getmountnodeobject(nodeindex)` — returns object — Gets the object mounted at a specific node.
`obj.getmountposition(index)` — returns string — Gets mount position `{x,y,z}` for mounted object.
`obj.getmounttransform(index)` — returns string — Gets mount transform for mounted object.
`obj.getnodeposition(nodename)` — returns string — Gets position of a named node on this shape.
`obj.getnodetransform(nodename)` — returns string — Gets full transform of a named node.
`obj.isininterior()` — returns bool — Is this object inside an interior?
`obj.ismounted()` — returns bool — Is this object mounted to something?
`obj.mountobject(target, node)` — returns bool — Mount this object to target at node.
`obj.unmount()` — Unmount from whatever this is mounted to.
`obj.unmountobject(target)` — returns bool — Unmount a specific object from this.

### Camera (3D)

`camera.updateposition()` — Update camera position based on current mode and follow target.

### fxSunLight (3D)

`fxsunlight.reset()` — Reset sun light animation to defaults.

### TRacerTrack

`track.setcheckpoints(checkpointstring)` — Set checkpoint positions for a race track.

### GameShapeDTS — Animation Transitions

`obj.isintransition()` — returns bool — Is this 3D shape currently in an animation transition?
